Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. A 16,000 × 525 RGB16 fixture encoded directly with libjxl reproduced PeerBrush’s two-minute JPEG XL open timeout. The blank document stayed intact. Native span decode now receives bounded, validated data under the worker deadline, then seals and replays the provisional spool after successful worker exit. Windows headless Open completed in 801.9 seconds with that change and 139.0 seconds in a subsequent replay with direct authenticated reads and row-level checks. The readback exposed a native sample mismatch on the fixture’s standard sRGB encoding. A 17 × 11 fixture reproduced the same mismatch. PeerBrush now uses its working profile when libjxl identifies decoded samples as structured standard sRGB and can adopt the already staged tiles when orientation is unchanged. Other encodings keep the existing profile handling. The Windows 0.4.0 replay opened the independent wide fixture in 40.6 seconds and matched all 33,600,000 native RGBA channel samples in the PNG readback. The small fixture also matched exactly, and both originals were preserved. These elapsed times came from separate diagnostic runs.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. Windows replay of the same 0.4.0 build exposed a display mismatch between the lossy RGB16 JPEG XL preview and its reopened document. Encoded previews and resident import now share native-depth, row-wise conversion of the actual decoded profile. Working sRGB goes directly to display projection. A fresh Windows replay passed RGB8 and RGB16 lossless and lossy export, exact preview comparison with the reopened display, and source and history protection.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. The native JPEG XL broker validates each rectangle’s cache identity once and retains authenticated chunk reads, cancellation checks and the final source rehash. An earlier Windows replay still hit the worker deadline on a 16,000 × 525 RGB16 export. The subsequent repair reads an aligned, sealed chunk directly into the admitted broker cache, checks ownership before and after the authenticated read, and starts the worker deadline at launch after guarded preparation. Queue waiting keeps its own deadline. The repaired Windows build completed the wide export in 728.0 seconds. An independent libjxl decoder and a separate editable PeerBrush reopen both matched all 33,600,000 native RGBA channel samples. The source project and exported bytes stayed intact.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. A later Windows 0.4.0 wide JPEG XL export reached the worker deadline before publication. The broker now checks source ownership at each requested rectangle boundary and around each physical chunk read. Copies from authenticated chunks already held in its private cache retain cancellation and cache-binding checks. Framed transfers keep transport validation and cancellation checks. Source checks bracket the exchange and verified log append, and the log verifies its own staging owner during writes. The final fresh source rehash still runs before publication. Runtime verification is pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-157 Windows headless verification checked exact raw-mask previews of offset RGB8 and RGB16 layers on 64 × 64 and 32 × 32 sampling grids over the same document rectangle. The replay covered enabled and disabled masks, including a hidden, partially opaque color layer with a Drop Shadow. Preview left the document and history intact. The desktop preference and GPU presentation were outside this replay.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-12. Native raw layer-mask previews stream directly from the selected mask without allocating a composition topology or preparing a display color plan. A layer without a mask fills its admitted preview stripe with white. The native sampling grid, projection, selection overlay, source admission and cancellation stay in use. Regression source checks exact output for offset RGB8 and RGB16 layers at full and reduced detail and checks that no composition topology or color stage is created. Runtime and timing verification remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-12. The native composite planner can skip color-empty tiles when layers have painted or feathered masks. It shares regional mask validation, checks the captured paint sources and prepares mask samples in the windows where the compositor needs them. A painted mask sample outside the color footprint leaves that region transparent. RGB8 and RGB16 regression source compares preview pixels and checks the export row planner with weighted paint, Gaussian feathering and a distant mask sample. Runtime and timing verification remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-12. Disk-backed masks split oversized output windows while keeping the full combined filter halo around each part. A 16,000-square layer can use stacked 64 px Gaussian feathers at RGB8 or RGB16. Actual scratch and source reads remain subject to the resident budget. Stacks whose halo can exceed the scratch ceiling for a single sample still use the previous admission limit. Regression source compares split and unsplit native bytes, exercises a full-width interior row at each depth and checks cleanup after a failed read. Runtime and full-document acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-12. PB-162 adds direct color-range swatches to Hue/Saturation and Selective Color and matching channel swatches to Levels, Curves, Channel Mixer and Blend If. Buttons expose their names to assistive technology and show them on hover. The selected name stays visible. Browsing ranges and channels is presentation state. Regression source clicks the Levels channel swatches in a narrow panel and checks that the original settings stay intact. Desktop acceptance remains pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-12. Whole-image filter presets have keyboard-selectable photo rows. The thumbnail worker shares the decoded reference photo, retains bounded results and moves on when a preset preview fails. The effect picker shows the same icons as the layer effect menu and preserves settings when the current type is chosen. Production Export uses matching full-canvas and tight-crop illustrations with the selected bounds mode visible. Regression source exercises a failed thumbnail followed by a valid photo. Desktop acceptance remains pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-12. PB-163 records the latest committed layer affine transform before the first Shift+D as well as after a copy. An active pixel selection takes priority. Folder descendants share their copied root's transform, and the new roots remain selected after one undoable duplication. Regression source checks repeated rotation, scale and translation at both bit depths and a nested multi-root selection. Desktop acceptance remains pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-12. PB-108 layer and mask thumbnails retry a temporary source inventory validation conflict after a short delay. The retry captures the current source again. Failed ownership and integrity checks remain visible. Regression source checks lock release, retry scheduling and preservation of genuine errors. Desktop reproduction and latency measurements remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-13. PB-108 resident layer and mask thumbnails share the committed frame snapshot across requests. Resident parameter previews share their derived result with the canvas and settings panels. Rendering can borrow an unchanged snapshot when its revision already matches. The clipboard worker opens the system clipboard on first use, and Windows creates its message owner on the first clipboard write. Startup and editing latency measurements remain pending.

Unreleased PB-108 update. Layer and mask thumbnails, the color picker and Magnetic Lasso now use sampling worker completion to wake the interface. A completed result retries shortly if the document lock is busy. Publication still checks the captured project, source and target. PeerBrush also starts its resident thumbnail worker on the first resident request, so opening a native document needs no resident thumbnail thread. Desktop timing remains unmeasured.

Unreleased PB-108 update. The Layers panel and canvas draw reservation indicators from the same document frame. Layer badges, menu availability, mask-link controls and canvas markers share that captured state. The engine checks current reservations when applying edits. Desktop timing remains unmeasured.

Unreleased PB-108 update. Native layer and mask thumbnails start their texture retirement workers when an image is ready to upload and retain them across document generations using the same resident budget. Idle retirement workers sleep until there is work or their last owner closes. Uploads still hold their memory charge until the renderer releases the image. Desktop timing remains unmeasured.

Unreleased PB-108 update. Finished native mask thumbnails carry across revisions of the same open document when their render inputs match. The Layers panel uses the full mask stack and its canvas placement. Inspector thumbnails use the layer-local stack through the selected step, so editing a later step leaves earlier thumbnails available. Pending work still cancels when the document generation changes. Desktop timing remains unmeasured.

Unreleased PB-108 update. Finished native color thumbnails for paint and Solid Color layers without active effects also carry across edits when their source, placement, canvas geometry, precision and display profile match. Layers with active effects and adjustment layers still render for the new revision. Desktop timing remains unmeasured.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-12. PB-108 canvas previews retain their last displayed pixels and cache when source inventory validation encounters a temporary lock. PeerBrush retries the same view after a short delay, or renders a changed view or edit immediately. The old frame cannot qualify as a current capture while the retry is pending. Regression source checks those transitions and the existing response to a hard error. Desktop verification remains pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-12. PB-108 native previews use session liveness and cancellation checks while projecting rows from pinned color and mask data. Full filesystem ownership validation still brackets source preparation and guards the handoff of each completed stripe. The source regression distinguishes a held inventory lock from known ownership loss. Runtime and latency measurements remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-12. PB-108 raw layer-mask previews and mask thumbnails reuse the shared liveness check while applying feather and mask operations to pinned paint samples. Each split window still validates its paint sources before reading and validates ownership again before returning its pixels. A failed source check during pinning retains its original error. Mask bake and PSD source paths keep their caller's full checks. Runtime and latency measurements remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-12. PB-108 normal composite previews use the same pinned-mask checks through both regional and stored layer windows. Whole-image filter previews validate every composite input window before reading and check ownership again before returning the completed filter output. CPU work on those pinned windows uses session liveness and cancellation. The cropped folder and adjustment regression source now includes a painted, feathered mask at both bit depths. Runtime and latency measurements remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-12. PB-108 pointwise mask thumbnails evaluate the requested grid at native precision, grouping samples by source tile. A thumbnail pins each contributing tile once per paint step. Cached and feathered prefixes still evaluate their full halo. Regression source compares the sampled factors bit for bit across tile boundaries and checks the rendered prefix thumbnails against the resident mask at both depths. Runtime and latency measurements remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-12. PB-108 cached mask thumbnails render nearby sample rows in shared windows and skip larger gaps between them. Each window retains the complete filter halo and splits within the regional scratch limit. The renderer packs only the requested 8-bit or 16-bit samples into the result before screen projection. Regression source compares sparse samples, frame edges and scratch-budget splits with the native dense render. Runtime and latency measurements remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-12. PB-162 brush tip angle uses a direction dial with exact degree entry. Brush and Pressure Calibration share a draggable graph of the engine's pressure response, with exact exponent entry. Dragging the midpoint keeps the minimum and maximum device-force settings in place. The Brush panel redraws the shared-engine stroke sample in the same frame as a parameter change. Regression source covers presentation-only animation and the final drag position. Desktop acceptance remains pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-12. PB-162 previews a hovered or keyboard-focused typeface in the text chooser. A bounded background worker renders a short specimen through the native text renderer with the exact bundled or installed face. Browsing keeps the draft unchanged until the user selects a row. Regression source checks distinct glyph output from two bundled weights and cancellation. Desktop acceptance remains pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-12. PB-162 records a new shortcut directly when the user presses a key, including Shift. The pointer chooser assigns the same combinations. The existing conflict and guarded file checks apply to both. The settings window shows the current Shift+D behavior and uses the Eraser and Pan icons for pen button roles. Regression source uses the actual recorder button and checks that conflicts and Escape preserve the binding and document. Desktop acceptance remains pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-12. PB-159 area selections retain the workspace press and recognize quick drags when the button-up arrives before a held-drag frame. Marquee bounds use the release position. Freehand and Quick Selection also consume motion that precedes the release in the same input batch. A canceled pointer contact discards the draft before processing a release. Regression source covers rotated and mirrored marquee starts and cancellation at both depths, plus a lasso that returns to its off-canvas starting point. Desktop acceptance remains pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-12. PB-159 rectangles, ellipses and gradients use the workspace press and exact release positions, including when both arrive in one input batch. One shared-engine command provides the live preview and the final edit. The gesture binds the document revision, layer and channel at press time. A changed source or canceled contact discards it. Regression source compares rotated and mirrored drags against the engine's native-depth pixels and checks one-step undo and source changes. Desktop acceptance remains pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-12. PB-159 brush, retouch and Liquify strokes use the workspace press, motion in input order and the exact release position. A simple click in the gray workspace leaves artwork intact. Touch button-up carries the reported end position, even when it differs from the last move. Paint uses genuine force samples from the press and moves and retains the last force for the release. Regression source covers rapid off-canvas strokes at both depths, including exact native pixels and undo. Desktop and hardware acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-12. PB-159 Pen anchors and handles can be placed or moved in the gray workspace. A quick handle drag uses the press and release positions even when later motion arrives in the same batch. Focus loss, contact cancellation and source changes discard the draft. Regression source covers a rotated and mirrored off-canvas anchor and exact handle geometry at both depths. Desktop acceptance remains pending.

Unreleased Windows development checks, 2026-11-12. With a disposable RGB8 photo on an earlier 0.4 build, photographic whole-image filter previews and the Canvas View popup were visible. A marquee started in the gray workspace committed at the canvas edge, and Shift+D created a layer from the selected pixels. On-canvas typing, moving a text draft with Free Transform and applying it as a text layer were observed. The Windows application build containing the Brush and Pen updates compiled. Desktop verification of those gestures on that build, repeated duplication and Photoshop acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-12. PB-108 transform geometry now retries transient source contention for the same document and targets. Canvas rendering uses its existing delayed retry for temporary project, workspace and backing admission conflicts. Stale sources and ownership failures still surface as errors. Regression source holds an actual project lock through geometry capture and checks that releasing it allows the same query to complete without editing. Desktop recovery and latency measurements remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-12. PB-162 gives Magic Wand matching scope separate icons for connected pixels and all matching pixels. Their tooltips and accessible labels explain which parts of the sampled image each setting can select. PB-163 now honors a recent whole-target transform when the pixel selection remains active after copying selected pixels. Each repeat copies the transformed layer in one history entry and keeps the marquee. Regression source covers two successive duplicates at both bit depths. Desktop acceptance remains pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-12. PB-159 Polygonal and Magnetic Lasso anchors can start or extend a path in the gray workspace. Magnetic Lasso keeps off-canvas clicks in order with pending on-canvas edge snaps. The shared selection engine clips coverage to the canvas and commits on close. Regression source covers mirrored and rotated views, clipped coverage and delayed snap order at both bit depths. Desktop acceptance remains pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-12. PB-162 shares the visual hue, saturation and brightness controls between the main swatches and the Text, shape and effect pickers. Main artwork swatches use one opacity control and exact HEX entry. Mask swatches use grayscale, including pasted HEX colors. Picker gestures use the actual release position and focused controls accept arrow keys. Each caller keeps its existing color encoding and precision. Regression source covers exact release position and keyboard adjustment. Desktop acceptance remains pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-12. PB-161 adds on-canvas text field edge and corner handles. Resize uses local text coordinates, keeps the opposite side fixed and previews the editable source through the shared engine. Escape or interrupted input restores the starting field without dropping the text draft. Regression source covers a transformed field under a rotated and mirrored view, exact release, cancellation, rendered preview and single-step Apply at both bit depths. Desktop acceptance remains pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-12. Dragging a new text field enables paragraph wrapping. The Text toolbar can switch wrapping for a draft. The resident and native renderers shape the same visual rows and keep the logical caret and selection indices through reflow. Existing legacy and shaped definitions retain their layout. Resident format 128 and the typed native source preserve paragraph mode alongside the standard pixels. Regression source covers word and grapheme boundaries, soft-wrap carets, resident and native rendering and guarded PSD/PSB reopening. Desktop acceptance remains pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-12. PB-108 lets a native pointer parameter preview finish while its gesture, target, source and sampling grid still match. New values coalesce without copying a second command payload or cancelling compatible rendering. Obsolete errors yield to the latest value. Release, cancellation and changes to the target or view invalidate the older request. Regression source drives an actual held UI control at native8/16, verifies latest-value capture under command-memory pressure and checks unchanged history. Runtime responsiveness and desktop acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-12. PB-108 applies the same scheduling to Free Transform handle drags on layers and editable text. Apply waits for the exact current transform frame after release. The native8/16 regression source checks intermediate presentation, final frame readiness and one-step commit. A Windows development build also refused an Eraser click during project contention. Native commands and undo/redo now retry transient source admission on their workers for up to one second. Retries use the captured source and stop on cancellation or source replacement. The updated build requires desktop verification.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-12. PB-108 lets Move, Rotate and Scale handles and Rectangle, Ellipse and Gradient drags show a completed native render while a compatible drag continues. The next request captures the latest position, and an ended drag invalidates the in-flight preview. Gizmo regression source checks the captured command, latest-value scheduling and invalidation. Desktop acceptance remains pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-12. Whole-image filter adjustments can show compatible in-flight frames on resident and disk-backed documents. The next request uses the current draft, and obsolete errors cannot block it. Applying a draft submits its current settings to the shared engine, including when the canvas is in a comparison view. Regression source drives the strength control and checks coalescing and the single committed edit at both native depths. Desktop acceptance remains pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-12. Text field placement and resizing can show completed renders during a compatible drag on resident and disk-backed documents. The canvas receives the updated native text command in the same input frame. Paragraph carets still require the exact current layout. Regression source checks intermediate presentation and the exact release width after later pointer motion, with one edit on Apply at native8 and native16. Desktop acceptance remains pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-12. On-canvas typing now lets a compatible preview finish and coalesces later input into the next render. After a 250 ms pause or focus loss, the current text settles without waiting for further input. The caret map still belongs to the exact rendered source draft. Regression source types two strings through the native canvas widget, checks progress and the settled frame, and then resizes and applies the same draft at both depths. Desktop acceptance remains pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-12. The desktop build enables eframe's AccessKit integration. Icon tool choices, swatches and Canvas View toggles expose their names and states, with visible keyboard focus. Accessible numeric actions use the existing edit path and preserve disabled controls and finite values. Regression source checks the app's accessibility tree and dispatches tool, view and range actions. Windows, macOS and Linux screen-reader acceptance remains pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. The Windows JPEG XL replay reproduced an inventory refusal during native export. The streaming worker supervisor and pump now serialize inventory checks within their shared source session. Both retain the existing ownership checks, and unrelated inventory work keeps its existing admission rules. The corrected Windows build passed RGB8 and RGB16 lossless and lossy export, preview, reopen and source-protection checks on a synthetic 32 × 24 document. Wide incremental decoding remains under verification.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. Preparing, previewing, accepting and retiring native proposals now take foreground source admission. Stored drafts release this admission between actions so the recovery worker can checkpoint live documents while a proposal awaits review. A Windows headless replay verified autosave for two RGB16 tabs with a pending draft, exact preview pixels, unchanged live pixels before acceptance, and one agent-attributed edit with exact native pixels after human acceptance.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. Native PSD/PSB Save computes the standard-data SHA-256 while it writes the separately sealed layer and composite spools. Assembly independently hashes the replayed spool bytes. Candidate readback compares original-depth samples, and publication verifies the candidate hash. Windows synthetic RGB8 and RGB16 saves reopened in both formats with exact standard raster and merged samples. Full-size timing remains pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-108 measured the time from process launch to the first authenticated headless RPC on an Intel Core i7-14650HX running Windows build 26200. Across eight alternating pairs, the published 0.30.1 Store build and a local release-profile 0.31.0 build with the Wand correction had medians of 102.41 ms and 58.24 ms with fresh isolated state, and 94.62 ms and 57.53 ms with reused isolated state. Package identity differs between the builds. OS caches and background load were uncontrolled. Desktop first frame, GPU setup, physical input and navigation latency remain unmeasured by this comparison.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-108/PB-157 color-managed resident RGB16 CPU previews convert bounded bands of native words directly into the display frame. Full bands preserve the ICC converter’s SIMD grouping, and GPU previews use the same bounded conversion on their native readback. Regression source compares full and cropped previews against one whole-frame matrix/TRC or LUT conversion. Runtime pixel and peak-memory verification remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-108/PB-157 resident RGB16 CPU display previews project each composited sample directly into the display frame. This removes the full RGBA16 staging frame from unprofiled composites, isolated layers and raw masks. Supported ICC conversion and GPU readback still use native words before projection. Native previews for clipboard, export and save keep their original sample depth. Regression source compares both preview grids and offset mask samples against native words. Runtime and memory measurements remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-108/PB-157 isolated resident mask previews retain only the selected mask for incremental updates. Brush previews reuse the displayed mask baseline and process the affected area with its filter halo. Unrelated layer effects no longer disable the mask’s regional path. Regression source compares RGB8/16 pixels against a complete render at full and reduced resolution, including disabled masks and the clamped edges of offset layers. Runtime and timing verification remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-157 shares the desktop preview mode for Wand parameters and refinement across resident and native canvases. Native Wand feather and tolerance edits now show the actual draft Cutout through the full-resolution preview path and the optional reduced-resolution path. Resident layer-mask refinement renders the requested raw mask directly. Resident selection projection samples rectangles and polygons directly and checks for supersession between rows. Regression source compares draft pixels at both depths, checks the return to full detail and verifies that preview leaves the source and history intact. Runtime acceptance remains pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-122 adds the shadow inspector’s Use Global Light checkbox and shared `effect.add` and `effect.update` link commands. The first link creates the document Global Angle in the same edit under a document-wide reservation. Unlinking retains the displayed direction. Soft shadows keep their pixel alignment preference. Regression source covers native history, authored standard style readback and command validation. Runtime and Photoshop acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-122 adds document-wide Global Light editing to the shared command engine and desktop project settings. The desktop control previews rendered shadows and commits the document angle and linked shadow settings in one history item. It keeps raster sources and selection coverage intact. Selective undo checks the resulting resource and links before publication. Regression source covers resident and native history, standard reopening and conflicts with later linked effects. Runtime and Photoshop acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-122 resolves supported modern and legacy hard shadows against an explicit Photoshop Global Angle resource. Standard PSD/PSB saves retain the link and the original local angle. Editing an individual shadow's direction detaches the link through shared effect settings. Resident format 122 binds the link and the standard descriptor to the document resource. Regression source covers both native depths, companion records, missing and mismatched resources, standard-only reopening, native writer readback and undo. Runtime and Photoshop acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-122 imports legacy hard Inner Shadow from version 0 and 2 effect records. The legacy parser reads signed 16.16 geometry and shares the modern renderer's bounds for local-light hard shadows. Legacy hard Drop Shadow is accepted alongside a matching modern definition with explicit source knockout. Masked or clipped shadows remain protected. Regression source covers both depths and record versions, standard-only PSD/PSB saves, companion geometry conflicts and saved-composite mismatch. Runtime and Photoshop acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-122 imports validated legacy RGB Color Overlays and their common state. A companion legacy overlay must match the modern definition's projection to legacy color words and byte opacity. The modern settings keep their original precision. Standalone legacy overlays use the shared editable style and standard save paths after exact composite admission. Regression source covers tag order, native words, conflicting records and standard-only PSD/PSB reopening. Runtime and Photoshop acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-122 Pattern Overlay shares Pattern Fill's bounded whole-pixel phase and right-angle placement codec. Both PSD/PSB writers retain supported placement in standard layer styles. Resident source format 121 binds placement and the shared asset. Native readback verifies both definitions and source channels. Regression source covers offset and rotation, an independent saved-composite oracle, mixed stacks, masks and shared assets. Runtime and Photoshop acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-122 saves ordered Gradient Overlay lists in PSD and PSB, including disabled entries and supported mixed style stacks. Both writers use one bounded descriptor encoder. The style reader admits a full 16-gradient list with maximal stops and keeps its existing byte and nesting limits. Resident source format 120 and native readback bind the ordered definitions. Regression source covers the shared 32-effect ceiling, exact settings, original channels and fallback. Runtime and Photoshop acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-122 emits supported Pattern Overlays as standard PSD/PSB styles in both writers, including mixed Pattern, Gradient and Color Overlay stacks. Asset admission is shared with Pattern Fill and preserves native RGB and alpha. A missing or unadmitted asset keeps the layer on the disclosed baked path. Resident source format 119 and native readback bind the descriptors and pattern bytes. Regression source covers both depths, masks, mixed stacks and asset mismatch. Runtime and Photoshop acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-122 emits a supported Gradient Overlay as a standard PSD/PSB style in both writers, including stacks with supported Color Overlays. The raster, mask and current merged composite retain native precision. Resident source format 118 and native readback bind the emitted descriptor. Regression source covers exact settings, linked and unlinked masks, original channels, descriptor mismatch and disclosed fallback. Runtime and Photoshop acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-108/PB-068 native selection refinement bounds its scalar and guide work to populated coverage tiles and their filter halos. It includes raw coverage beyond legacy bounds hints, keeps tile-aligned filter arithmetic and uses the original canvas boundaries. Profiled guides retain the original row width. Geometry history snapshots skip blank exterior rows. Regression source compares cropped and full-frame refinement on an offset soft mask. Runtime and latency measurements remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-108/PB-068 native contiguous Quick Selection and Object Selection stage their image guides within the search area. Profiled guides preserve the original row width for exact display conversion. The Quick Selection flood uses search-local storage. Feathering and combination with an existing selection retain document coordinates. The coverage writer tracks populated tiles and skips scanning and clearing empty tile buffers. Regression source covers crop planning, exact coverage across a translated search and empty tiles across successive tile rows. Runtime and latency measurements remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-108 native effect staging, edge guides and stripe rendering share one captured composition topology per render. Accepted texture reuse carries the same topology from eligibility to sampling, and releases it before final upload assembly. The renderer verifies scene and target scope before emitting stripes. Regression source covers scope refusal and selection stripe output and admission. Runtime and latency measurements remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-108 raw folder and adjustment previews prepare only their reachable layer dependencies. Pass-through backdrops, clipped adjustments and Blend If inputs follow the compositor's existing rules. Clipping base indices are resolved with the topology and reused for adjustment samples. RGB8/16 regression source compares the scoped output to full preparation and checks that unrelated layer reads are rejected. Runtime and latency measurements remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-108/PB-068 stored previews and composite output now propagate the crop through folders and adjustments. Earlier stages keep the backdrop and halo required by later effects. A global backdrop dependency requests a full input while its own output can remain cropped. RGB8/16 regression source compares raw folder previews and composites with local blur, smoothed shadows and an adjustment against full-frame preparation. It also checks retained disk use. Runtime and latency measurements remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-108/PB-068 cropped smoothed styles now read only the source rows and columns from the original origin through the crop and smoothing halo. The smoothing sums keep their original operation order. Layer-aligned gradients use a separate full-source alpha reduction when required. RGB8/16 regression source compares cropped field bits against full emission and checks the source footprint, disk usage and retirement. Runtime and latency measurements remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-108/PB-068 cropped smoothed styles retain only the scalar field samples required by the stored output and its halo. The full-origin smoothing order and full-source alpha scan are preserved. The temporary file binds its complete cropped payload and restores logical coordinates on tile reads. RGB8/16 regression source compares exact scalar bits, checks disk admission and rejects reads beyond the retained footprint. Runtime and latency measurements remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-108/PB-068 cropped native raster previews, copies, edge guides and flattened exports can retain bounded stored effect outputs. Each local stage includes its successor's input halo, and document filters expand the composite crop before reading layer stages. Smoothed fields and layer-wide statistics retain their full inputs. Folders and adjustments retain full backdrop preparation. Regression source compares cropped RGB8/16 Liquify and mixed shadow/blur stacks with full output and checks rejection of reads outside the prepared crop. Runtime and latency measurements remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-108 native composite previews, Copy Merged and flattened export prepare the layers reachable through the compositor's visible groups and clipping bases. Hidden folder descendants and followers of hidden clipping bases skip color staging. Providers verify their preparation scope before sampling. PSD layer-channel saves use the full-layer scope. Regression source checks exact RGB8/16 preview, PNG and clipboard output under a disk budget below the hidden shadow stages. Runtime and latency measurements remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-108/PB-110 native single-layer Copy prepares the selected raster layer and its mask. Mask Copy reads the selected raw mask through the regional engine, including disabled samples. Copy Merged, folder and adjustment copies retain their compositing dependencies. Regression source exercises hidden clipped RGB8/16 layers and masks under a disk budget that refuses full-canvas shadow staging, with exact samples, bounds and cleanup checks. Runtime and latency measurements remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-108 scopes native raster layer color previews, thumbnails and edge guides to the requested layer's preparation. Hidden and clipped layers retain their raw preview appearance. Folder, adjustment and composite requests retain their compositing dependencies. Regression source compares native RGB8/16 pixels against full preparation and checks that unrelated stored stages are absent during streaming. Runtime and latency measurements remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-108/PB-157 raw layer-mask previews prepare only the requested mask. Native requests bypass stored color stages and prepare the mask through the shared regional mask engine; resident RGB8/16 previews prepare only that layer's mask before sampling. Disabled masks retain their raw samples, missing masks remain white, and original crop/grid/precision rules are unchanged. Regression source covers hidden offset layers, weighted painted/blurred disabled masks, unrelated layers, exact resident/native output, no color-stage disk use during streaming, cancellation and unchanged document sources. Runtime/GPU and latency acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-108/PB-157 selection Mask, Cutout and Overlay projections use the native renderer's captured sampling grid. Rounded short crop dimensions no longer alter coverage sampling, and native16 projection retains the renderer's double-precision coordinates. Resident projection derives scale from the longest source edge at the matching channel depth. Regression source anchors coverage to rendered grayscale artwork across wide/tall offset crops, reduced/full grids and both native depths. Document sources remain unchanged. Compile-only and runtime qualification are separate; runtime/GPU acceptance remains pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-122 writes one supported solid Stroke as an editable standard PSD/PSB style on unmasked Normal paint layers. Both writers preserve inside, center or outside placement, whole-pixel size through 128, exact color/opacity and enabled state alongside original RGB8/16 channels and the current merged image. Supported stacks retain Color Overlays, Inner Shadow, Stroke and Drop Shadow in Photoshop execution order; active exterior Stroke plus Drop Shadow keeps the rendered fallback. Resident source format 117 binds the descriptor. Regression source covers both save paths, descriptor conflicts, disabled styles, capacity and unsupported settings/order. Runtime and Photoshop verification remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-122 writes supported Color Overlay stacks followed by one hard Inner Shadow and one hard Drop Shadow in Photoshop execution order. The writers use `lfx2` for singleton overlay families and `lmfx` when multiple Color Overlays are present. Original paint channels and the current merged image remain separate. Resident source format 116 binds the stack order. RGB8/16 regression source covers both layouts, disabled overlays and refusal of unsupported ordering. Runtime and Photoshop verification remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-122 writes eligible hard Inner and Drop Shadows as editable PSD/PSB styles in both save paths. The supported single-style paint layers retain their original raster bounds and channels. The standard descriptor carries local light angle, distance, linear contour and Drop Shadow knockout, while the merged image includes the rendered shadow. Resident source format 115 binds the descriptor. RGB8/16 regression source covers both shadow types and rendered fallback for blur. Runtime and Photoshop verification remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-122 writes eligible Color Overlay stacks as one ordered standard `lmfx` record in resident and disk-backed PSD/PSB saves. The list retains disabled entries and preserves original layer channels and a current merged image. Single overlays keep their existing `lfx2` bytes. Resident source format 114 binds the order, settings and enabled state. RGB8/16 regression source covers masked fills, offset paint and refusal of reordered definitions. Runtime and Photoshop verification remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-122 writes eligible paint layers with original PSD/PSB raster bounds and channels plus an editable standard Color Overlay. The merged image still renders the overlay. Resident source format 113 binds the original frame and style, and native readback retains the prior rendered frame when reopening older mixed saves. RGB8/16 regression source covers offset paint, partial alpha, hidden RGB, exact composite and rendered fallback. Runtime and Photoshop verification remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-122 keeps standard editable Color Overlay on eligible Solid Color fills with byte-exact layer opacity. Non-Normal layer blends also remain editable when the layer carries an explicit Blend Interior Effects As Group record. Resident source format 112 binds the standard layer blend, opacity and grouping along with the overlay. Regression source includes both depths, an opaque backdrop, a grouped Multiply layer, altered grouping and rendered fallback for ungrouped or inexact settings. Runtime and Photoshop verification remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-122 preserves disabled Color Overlays and unlinked raster masks on eligible Solid Color fills in both PSD/PSB writers. Unlinked masks retain the explicit `lmgm` mask-shaping mode. Resident source format 111 binds these states. Regression source covers disabled styles, unlinked soft coverage, editable reopen and refusal when standard mask compositing bytes change. Runtime and Photoshop verification remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-122 saves eligible Solid Color fills with an editable Color Overlay and linked raster mask in both PSD/PSB writers. The standard base channels, `lfx2` style, `-2` mask and current merged image remain available. Resident source format 110 binds the masked layout. RGB8/16 regression source covers soft coverage, linked-mask reopen and unlinked-mask fallback. Runtime and Photoshop verification remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-122 uses one Photoshop blend descriptor table for reading styles and writing eligible Color Overlays on Solid Color fills. Resident and disk-backed PSD/PSB saves preserve the overlay blend, opacity and RGB values with the original base channels and a current merged image. Resident source format 109 binds the blended records while retaining the earlier Normal-only plans. Regression source covers canonical identifiers, legacy aliases, native8 Multiply, native16 Vivid Light and edited descriptor refusal. Runtime and Photoshop verification remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-122 writes standard Color Overlay for partial opacity and fractional RGB on the supported Solid Color fills when the descriptor values round-trip exactly. Both save paths retain original layer channels and render a current merged image. Resident source format 108 binds the wider subset while format 107 retains its earlier rules. Native readback accepts the earlier mix of standard and rendered overlays. RGB8/16 regression source covers both formats, exact settings and appearance, descriptor conflicts and fallback for fractional effect weight. Runtime and Photoshop verification remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-122 resident PSD/PSB saves share the native writer's standard Color Overlay subset. The resident writer keeps the original Solid Color channels and the rendered merged image, and source format 107 binds both standard definitions to the editable source. Compatibility reporting follows the actual save plan. RGB8/16 regression source covers standard-only and embedded reopen, altered descriptor rejection and partial-opacity fallback. Runtime and Photoshop verification remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-122 disk-backed PSD/PSB saves write an editable standard Solid Color fill and Color Overlay style for an opaque, unclipped, unmasked fill with one full-strength Normal overlay and byte-exact RGB. The standard channels retain the base color, and the current merged image retains the rendered overlay. Source36 readback compares the style record and channels. Other settings use the existing rendered-layer path. RGB8/16 regression source covers PSD/PSB conventional reopen, descriptor conflicts and fallback. Photoshop and runtime verification remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-122 recognizes Photoshop style descriptor IDs for Darker Color, Lighter Color, Vivid Light, Linear Light, Pin Light, Hard Mix, Hue, Saturation, Color and Luminosity, plus the standard Subtract and Divide spellings. The shared native-depth compositor must still reproduce the saved image before editing is available. Regression source covers RGB8/16 PSD and PSB import, mismatched composites and editable reopen in PeerBrush. Photoshop and runtime verification remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-108/PB-110 the shared native clear command used by Cut skips tiles that have no raster data or cannot contain selection coverage. It still materializes a nominal fill when required and prepares coverage only over the clipped intersection for edited tiles. Native8/16 regression source covers color and raw mask clears, unchanged hidden RGB, retained tile references and exact soft alpha. Runtime and timing remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-108/PB-110 Mask Cut reuses Copy's sparse span planning for the raw paint step. Missing artwork or coverage tiles bypass sample work, and selected hidden RGB still reaches the clipboard. Native8/16 regression source compares raw clipboard output, sparse tile retention and unchanged source authority. Runtime and timing remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-108/PB-110 selected Copy and Copy Merged intersect artwork spans with the physical coverage tiles. Missing mask tiles remain transparent even when the artwork is dense. Native8/16 regression source compares exact clipboard output and origin for both commands under a sample allowance below one full row. Runtime and timing remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-108/PB-110 selection previews use the sampled extent of each band to skip missing coverage tiles. Selection Mask writes opaque black for empty spans without sampling them. Raw coverage outside the bounds hint remains visible. Regression source compares exact and reduced grids with separated fractional samples under a coverage work allowance below the full row. Runtime and timing remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-108/PB-110 streams the native Selection Mask view directly from selection coverage. The view keeps the existing sampling grid and skips artwork compositing, derived effect staging and display color conversion. Regression source compares exact and reduced grids, including a profiled crop that can show the mask when color preview admission is exhausted. Runtime and large-canvas timing remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-155/PB-157 uses exclusive first publication for preview, tool, shortcut and default-editor settings. A file created after the final comparison keeps its exact bytes. Updates to an existing file still require the bytes that were loaded, and failed saves leave the active preference unchanged. Regression source injects a competing file at publication and checks retry after reloading. Runtime verification remains pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-070/PB-122 retains the standard four-byte Photoshop Global Altitude resource through resident and disk-backed PSD/PSB workflows. Resident source format 106 binds the value to editable metadata. Regression source covers both native depths, ordinary edit/save, duplicate and short records, and conflicting standard and embedded values. Global Light-dependent style rendering and native runtime verification remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-080/PB-138 rechecks the resident document after the staged autosave PSD is flushed and holds the matching dirty revision through publication. The recovery record can finish after the PSD is visible, while encoding and staging leave the document available. Regression source saves the original during staging and checks that the earlier recovery remains intact without an unpaired PSD. Runtime verification remains pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-070/PB-122 retains Photoshop's standard Global Angle resource in both PSD/PSB writers and binds it to editable sources. RGB8/16 regression source covers the valid endpoints, edits and reopen, duplicates, malformed values and a standard-resource change against unchanged source metadata. Global Light-dependent styles, runtime and Photoshop verification remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-070/PB-122 preserves Photoshop's Copyright Flag in resident and native PSD/PSB workflows. Standard saves retain explicit true or false, and editable sources must agree with the standard resource. Regression source covers edits, both writers, duplicate and malformed resources, and conflicting source metadata at native 8- and 16-bit depth. Runtime and Photoshop verification remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-156/PB-023 dispatches Ctrl/Cmd+E through the same tool shortcut guards as custom bindings. Tool selection and transform mode switches require a fresh key press, so held Eraser keys cannot clear a new stroke draft. Repeat behavior remains available for brush-size controls. Regression source covers the command alias and a custom Eraser key, draft preservation and unchanged history. Runtime and native keyboard verification remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-157 renders native raster fades on the full-detail grid even when the observed starting canvas was reduced. A matching full-detail baseline can still be reused. A reduced starting frame binds the observed source and view, and the worker renders both snapshots on the same full-detail grid. RGB8/16 regression source covers a reduced starting frame with the opt-in active and verifies multiple full-detail fade publications. Native GPU and latency verification remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-110/PB-108 sends zero-feather selection rows directly to the scalar writer. The writer skips zero tile segments, and contour generation skips a segment only when it and the preceding row are both zero. Regression source covers unfeathered combine, a contour that crosses a tile seam and closes on the next empty row, and a distant soft sample below the outline threshold. Runtime and timing remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-110/PB-108 uses the affine tile footprint for sparse rasterized selection coverage. Empty mask tiles bypass scalar sampling while the shared writer still records exact soft values and regenerates bounds and contours. Regression source checks two separated fractional samples, their contours, and an empty gap under a work allowance below the full selection area. Runtime and latency remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-110/PB-108 skips empty source footprints during selected native affine transforms. The tile check includes the complete bilinear footprint, with conservative bounds for rounded shear. The cut still clears only selected source pixels. RGB8/16 regression source checks a half-pixel sample across a tile seam, retained hidden RGB outside the selection, shared tile identity and the untouched original under a bounded work allowance. Runtime and timing remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-110/PB-108 uses native composite tile coverage for selected Copy Merged. Empty columns and bands remain transparent without spending per-sample work. Clipboard dimensions, document origin, native precision and transaction retirement are retained. RGB8/16 regression source covers a wide selection with distant pixels and a work allowance below the full rectangle's sampling cost. Runtime clipboard and latency verification remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-158 checks APT client trust continuity during signature attachment and publication-stage readback. Update candidates must match the preceding approved primary fingerprint and client keyring bytes. Regression source covers a changed fingerprint, a changed keyring, and refusal to contact the public endpoint when the staged replacement matches its own receipt but differs from the previous keyring. Native and public acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-158 binds the exact Scoop, Homebrew, APT and Nix release filenames through public delivery, native install and upgrade readbacks. The public catalogs also reconcile the corresponding source archive's byte count. Regression source rejects a renamed download even with matching bytes, disagreement between native filenames, and inconsistent public source sizes. Native acceptance and public delivery remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-108 extends source-bound paint damage through Stroke and unsmoothed spatial styles. Full-origin smoothed styles retain their existing path. Regression source covers exact native8/16 preview output around the outline and hard shadow, combined layer and filter halos, packed partial rendering, and unchanged history. Runtime GPU and latency verification remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-122 aligns the editor and shared Stroke validation with the Photoshop import limit of 128 pixels. PSD and PSB import still requires a supported descriptor and an exact original-depth saved composite. RGB8/16 regression source covers maximum-size import, native preview and export fringes, editable save and reopen, and protection above the limit. Runtime and Photoshop verification remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-068/PB-108 renders hard shadows and other unsmoothed spatial styles in regional native windows. Fractional casts use the same document-coordinate arithmetic as the full frame. Built-in Drop Shadow and Outer Glow contours can use sparse coverage through their full halo. RGB8/16 regression source checks a fractional shadow across a tile seam, exact export and crop, and preview parity. Runtime and timing remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-122 retains supported Photoshop Outside and Center Stroke with a hard Drop Shadow when either effect is disabled, either opacity is zero, or Stroke size is zero. Both definitions are validated and the original-depth composite must still match. Simultaneously contributing exterior strokes and drop shadows stay protected. RGB8/16 regression source checks enabled and dormant definitions, native raster pixels and both save formats. Runtime and Photoshop verification remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-068/PB-108 includes Stroke in sparse native preview and export planning. The tile map includes its complete halo, including across tile boundaries. The shared renderer dispatches Stroke directly and builds only the distance maps required by its position. Regression source compares the sparse composite with the full layer preview, checks exported native8/16 alpha at the edge and checks exact coverage for Inside, Outside and Center. Runtime and timing remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-068/PB-108 uses bounded horizontal effect windows for live native rendering as well as stored stages. A stroke's halo and preceding source effects are rendered in document coordinates before the pieces are assembled into a complete output band. Regression source compares a wide native 8/16-bit Stroke preview under a 16 MiB resident budget with the resident renderer. Runtime and memory measurements remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-068/PB-108 splits wide stored effect stages with a spatial halo into bounded horizontal windows. Each window still reads its complete halo and uses the same global style fields. Output is assembled in the original row order. Regression source covers native 8/16-bit Stroke across a window boundary under a 16 MiB resident budget. Runtime, timing and Photoshop acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-122 admits supported hard Inner Shadow before a solid Stroke and supports a hard Drop Shadow beneath an Inside Stroke. Both use the original casting alpha and still require an exact saved-composite match. Outside and Center Stroke with Drop Shadow remain protected. Synthetic RGB8/16 regression source covers the effect order and reopening both save formats. Runtime and Photoshop round-trip verification remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-122 imports one supported solid Photoshop Stroke above supported overlays when the complete saved composite matches at the original precision. A synthetic RGB8/16 regression checks effect order, preserved source pixels, both save formats and protection after a composite mismatch. Stroke combined with shadows remains protected. Runtime and Photoshop round-trip verification remain pending. [Krita layer style projection](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/KDE/krita/master/libs/image/layerstyles/kis_layer_style_projection_plane.cpp)

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-122 checks the original alpha of supported solid Photoshop Strokes during native PSD/PSB row decoding. Native Open can retain the editable Stroke beyond the resident canvas limit after an exact saved-composite match. Partial source alpha stays protected. Regression source covers wide RGB8/16 opens, a one-sample mismatch and partial alpha. Indexed parsing also retains both standard print-flag records and reserves their IDs in earlier large-file fixtures. Runtime and Photoshop acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-155 routes shared preference reads through the existing native regular-file guard. This closes the check-before-open link race and prevents a Unix FIFO from blocking startup or settings checks. Bounded reads and missing-file defaults still apply. The existing Unix filesystem regression now exercises the shared preference entry point. Compilation, native filesystem and runtime acceptance remain separate checks.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-070/PB-122 retains Photoshop Print Flags and version 1 Print Flags Information in resident and native documents. PSD/PSB saves emit both standard resources and bind them to editable sources. Malformed, duplicate and conflicting records stay protected. Regression source covers edits, both decoders and writers, wide native Open and compatible copy. Runtime and receiving-application verification remain pending. [Adobe Photoshop file format specification](https://www.adobe.com/devnet-apps/photoshop/fileformatashtml/)

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-11. PB-068/PB-108 bounds sparse resize and other native region lookups by choosing between the stored tile list and the requested tile positions. Native Image Size reuses horizontal coordinates across each output tile and writes 8-bit samples directly. The same native-depth samplers, guarded source rows and per-operation work limit apply. The existing 16000-square resize regression now checks clipped occupancy queries on both source and result. Runtime and timing remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-10. PB-068/PB-108 merges sorted native raster patches into the immutable tile index in one pass. The index retains its verified disk pool so viewport and save admission can check membership without scanning every tile owner again. Existing reference pins still verify backing data. Regression source covers insertion, replacement, removal, untouched references and refusal of a foreign disk pool. Runtime and timing remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-10. PB-108 bounds native viewport occupancy scans to visible tiles and the layer, adjustment and filter halos that can reach them. Sparse sources still use their physical index when it is cheaper to scan. Whole-document export keeps full coverage. Regression source compares native 8/16-bit crops with shifted blur halos against full coverage and resident output. Runtime and timing remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-10. PB-070/PB-122 retains the Photoshop layer ID seed alongside existing layer IDs. Both PSD/PSB writers preserve its four standard bytes and bind the value to supplementary sources. Duplicate, malformed or conflicting records protect the original at its native channel depth. Regression source covers layer duplication, edited metadata, native Save and wide Open. Runtime and receiving-application verification remain pending. [Adobe Photoshop file format specification](https://www.adobe.com/devnet-apps/photoshop/fileformatashtml/)

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-10. PB-070/PB-122 retains the Photoshop version 1 grid record when there are no guides. Both writers preserve the standard bytes and bind them to supplementary sources. Files with guides or invalid records keep the saved native-depth composite read-only. Regression source covers both writers, an altered record, and wide native Open and compatible copy. Runtime and receiving-application verification remain pending. [Adobe Photoshop file format specification](https://www.adobe.com/devnet-apps/photoshop/fileformatashtml/)

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-10. PB-068/PB-108 native transform bounds scan each row from both ends and reuse measured bounds for repeated verified blocks of the same clipped size. Work is charged for the samples inspected. Original-source bounds include hidden RGB, while visible-content bounds use native alpha. Regression source covers repeated native16 tiles and clipped padding on a 16000-square canvas. Runtime and timing remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-10. PB-068/PB-108 native exact flips, quarter-turns and integer translations copy clipped native samples directly. The fast path handles source tile boundaries and avoids the floating-point coordinate buffer. It meters up to four byte-exact pixel transfers per work unit and keeps per-row cancellation and shared memory and disk limits. Regression source covers a 16000-square quarter-turn and resident byte parity for reflected and rotated output at both channel depths. Runtime and timing remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-10. PB-068/PB-108 native tile-aligned translations copy verified whole tiles without per-pixel mapping and reuse identical blocks in the destination store. Exact same-frame identity preserves physical padding, and moved or clipped edges still use the sampler. Each result owns its output independently of the original. Regression source covers a 16000-square source with hidden native16 samples, a blank tile and nonzero old edge padding. Runtime and timing remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-10. PB-068/PB-108 builds the retained source for a native solid fill with up to four guarded tile writes. Each distinct edge shape retains exact native samples and zero padding. The work meter still charges for each logical tile and the store owner is checked before handoff. Regression source covers a 16000-square fill source at both depths. Transforms still admit their separate resampling work. Runtime and timing remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-10. PB-068/PB-108 native stroke planning stores dab groups only for touched tiles. The completed plan releases the unused part of its temporary memory reservation. Shared paint, erase and sampled tools retain row-major tile order and stroke-order dab coverage. Regression source exercises a returning stroke along the bottom of a 16000-square canvas and checks retained memory admission. Runtime and input-to-display measurements remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-10. PB-108 uses one guarded scan of the sealed layer and composite spools to bind the native Photoshop manifest. Assembly independently checks the combined section digest on the replayed bytes and skips unused per-section hashes. The whole-file seal and readback remain in place. Regression source also covers a changed composite byte during replay. Save timing remains pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-10. PB-158 now reconciles the corresponding source archive across public, clean-install and upgrade readbacks for each package channel. The APT stage and all native readbacks emit the bound source hash. The delivery join also requires the Scoop desktop and Connect AI evidence for both lifecycle checks. Regression source covers mixed source archives and missing desktop evidence. Native qualification and public delivery remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-10. PB-158 permits native APT clean-install qualification of a current signed repository generation with retained history. The install harness verifies the authenticated predecessor without installing its package and records that pin for offline readback and archived baselines. This lets clean-install and upgrade evidence select the same release. Native qualification and public delivery remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-10. PB-070 binds supplementary editable PSD/PSB sources to the standard ICC resource. Native save verifies its working profile on readback. Adding or removing the built-in sRGB tag while keeping the old supplementary metadata leaves a read-only composite. Non-working embedded profiles continue through protected import. Regression source covers 8-bit PSD and 16-bit PSB using real native save and Open entry points. Runtime and receiving-application verification remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-10. PB-070 retains Photoshop's intentionally untagged profile flag through editable PSD and PSB opens and saves. Both writers include the standard record, and supplementary sources must agree with it. Conflicting ICC, duplicate or malformed declarations keep the original read-only. Regression source covers both channel depths and large native Open. Runtime and receiving-application verification remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-10. PB-070 retains the standard Photoshop square-pixel aspect record in editable PSD and PSB files. Resident and native saves bind the record to their supplementary source metadata. Non-square, malformed and duplicate records leave the file read-only at its original channel depth, with no display stretching. Regression source covers both standard record versions and native 8/16-bit saves. Runtime and receiving-application verification remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-10. PB-070 protects PSD/PSB documents that declare global hidden effects or contain Photoshop Layer Comps or Timeline data. The readers keep the saved native-depth composite available and refuse editable restoration from supplementary sources. A single explicit effects-visible flag keeps normal editable behavior. Regression source covers conventional and supplementary records at both depths. Runtime and receiving-application verification remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-10. PB-070 keeps duplicate Photoshop Version Info records visible to the bounded PSD/PSB decoder and rejects ambiguous or invalid merged-image declarations. Resident, indexed preview and native supplementary paths share prefix validation. Regression source covers reversed duplicates, a truncated prefix and an invalid flag at both channel depths. Runtime and receiving-application verification remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-10. PB-118 lets the desktop preview select any planned export output. The panel reuses the exact source-bound plan across selections and keeps the selector available when one output cannot render. Regression source compares the second size variant with the matching protocol preview at both channel depths. Runtime acceptance remains pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-10. PB-118 handles export worker launch refusal. Batch registration and receipt eviction happen only after a successful launch, and the file-operation guard is released on failure. The desktop preview enters its pending state only after its worker starts. Regression source injects refusal and checks preserved receipts, successful retry and one-batch admission. Runtime acceptance remains pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-10. PB-118 binds desktop batch export to the exact native document generation, using the same observation as desktop preview. A replaced source with the same document ID and revision is refused before job registration. CLI and MCP retain their existing request fields and use the same batch worker and publication guards. Regression source checks the refusal and absence of files or a run at both native depths. Runtime acceptance remains pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-10. PB-118 binds desktop Production Export previews to the exact native document generation and target mode. A source replacement with the same document ID and revision invalidates the preview and rejects stale work before rendering. Switching from an empty layer selection to composite clears the error and refreshes the preview. Regression source covers both native depths. Runtime acceptance remains pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-10. PB-072 keeps the last pointer movement of an active canvas stroke when it shares a frame with release. The movement uses its matching force, or the last held force if none arrived. Later hover cannot extend the stroke. Regression source compares the exact 8/16-bit committed result with the shared engine. Runtime and physical tablet verification remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-10. PB-072 ties touch pointer events to the owning device and contact. Cancellation of that contact discards unfinished brush, Liquify and calibration drafts. The adapter emits a pointer exit without synthesizing a click. Regression source covers an unrelated cancellation, a canceled stroke, a late release and a fresh dab. Runtime and physical tablet verification remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-10. PB-072 preserves contact pressure for taps that start and end in the same frame. Canvas and calibration input take force at the pointer position, and a fresh mouse stroke uses full force. Regression source covers native-depth artwork and calibration previews. Physical tablet and runtime verification remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-10. PB-080/PB-138 lists unpaired resident PSDs as separate recovery candidates. An explicit restore rechecks the file identity and exact bytes, requires a valid embedded PeerBrush document, and opens a new unsaved project. The Recovery window can also rebuild the missing record and return the version to normal autosave rotation. It preserves the PSD and any competing record. Regression source covers exact native16 recovery, changed candidates, conventional PSDs without embedded sources and record repair. Runtime verification remains pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-10. PB-080/PB-138 publishes resident recovery PSDs and commit records with exclusive moves. A late file at either destination is preserved. An unpaired final PSD stays on disk and triggers the incomplete-inventory warning, while cleanup can still remove generated temporary files. Regression source injects competing writes at both publication boundaries and checks discovery after an interrupted record write. Runtime verification remains pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-10. PB-080/PB-138 reports incomplete resident recovery inventories in the chooser and recovery list response. Readable versions remain available. Automatic resident writes and rotation pause when a generated marker or its payload cannot be accounted for. Recovery reads use guarded file handles. Regression source covers an occupied marker, a second valid recovery and retry after the marker is restored. Runtime verification remains pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-10. PB-080/PB-138 validate the bytes of resident recovery versions before they can displace older checkpoints. The existing record checksum is streamed in 64 KiB chunks. If rotation cannot verify a version it would keep, the old checkpoints and the newly published version are retained, and another write waits for safe retention. Regression source covers same-size damage and resumed rotation after explicit discard. Runtime verification remains pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-10. PB-080/PB-138 restrict resident autosave orphan cleanup to filenames the writer generates and preserve recovery PSDs when the version record is occupied or cannot be inspected. Publication errors leave the PSD for that same check, including when a record became visible before a sync failed. Regression source covers incomplete files, similarly named backups and recovery of exact native16 pixels after the record becomes available. Runtime verification remains pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-10. PB-110 cancels a deferred selection shortcut when a newer keyboard selection or history command arrives. The check runs before worker completion can replay the earlier shortcut. Regression source queues Cut and Move against a committed one-pixel selection and verifies that a newer Deselect preserves the artwork and current tool at both native depths. Runtime and desktop acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-10. PB-110 preserves shortcuts pressed before a keyboard selection change in the same input batch. A later Cut, Copy or selection-dependent transform requires a fresh press so it cannot use earlier coverage. Regression source covers layer Copy before Select All, a blocked Cut and Move, and a fresh pixel Copy at both native depths. Runtime and desktop acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-10. PB-110 binds shortcuts that arrive during a selected move or transform to the worker's committed selection. The shared classification also covers path-to-selection and edits that clear coverage, including canvas geometry changes. History operations are treated as potentially changing coverage. If the committed selection is empty, deferred shortcuts are skipped. Regression source copies after a native Move release and pastes at the new document coordinates at both depths. Runtime and desktop reproduction remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-10. PB-110 keeps selection shortcuts from attaching to the wrong gesture when a second canvas press shares the release frame, or when an earlier selection worker still owns the pending result. Copy and transform shortcuts in those input batches require a fresh press. Regression source covers native-depth artwork preservation, clipboard dispatch and pending-worker event routing. The reported physical recurrence and runtime acceptance remain open.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-10. PB-108 batches PackBits row-table reads during PSD/PSB indexing. The indexer and composite plane verifier share the same bounded count scanner. Each count is still validated, and the total must cover the encoded payload exactly. Regression source checks both table widths, bounded read counts and a changed count that breaks payload coverage. Runtime and open-time measurements remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-10. PB-108 and PB-068 now use the single-render PSD/PSB comparison for ZIP and ZIP Prediction when the decoded planes fit in at most 64 MiB and one quarter of the currently available resident budget. The decoder validates the full compressed stream, then compares native samples from the admitted buffer. Larger composites use sequential verification. No disk spool is needed. Regression source extends the wide Gradient Fill fixture to PackBits, ZIP and ZIP Prediction and includes a one-word ZIP Prediction mismatch. Runtime and open-time measurements remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-10. PB-108 and PB-068 extend single-render PSD/PSB Open qualification to PackBits saved composites. The decoder checks that the row table partitions the full payload into bounded planes, decodes each plane with the shared PackBits reader, and compares original-depth samples. When the additional packed-row working memory is unavailable, Open uses the sequential comparison. ZIP and ZIP Prediction use the sequential path. Regression source covers both table widths, incomplete reads and a changed row count. Runtime and open-time measurements remain pending.
