Grouped overlays preserve original raster samples, the layer blend and the editable effect. Clipping groups and other style families with a non-Normal layer blend remain protected. Regression source covers RGB8/16 Multiply and Screen, hidden RGB, tag order, flag refusal, exact composite checks and PSD/PSB saves. Photoshop fixture and receiving-editor acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-10. Cross-tab drops use the primary-button release position and its Shift modifier. Changing the hover target or preview quality cancels obsolete work, and a drop waits for the hover worker to release its source session before preparing the edit. Cancellation reaches native staging, tile reads and rendering. Resident renders check cancellation before and after the render call. Regression source covers batched pointer departure, queued drop ordering, and native cancellation after the coarse frame with disk cleanup. Runtime latency and installed input acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-10. Layer copies and Shift-moves now work between disk-backed tabs. The same source fence checks both captured revisions, history cursors and current reservations before publishing either side. A successful move creates one independent undo entry in each tab and shares existing tile owners when precision permits. Regression source covers rendered hover, a late destination reservation, exact native16 source reuse and independent source undo. Installed acceptance remains pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-10. Disk-backed tabs accept layer copies and Shift-moves from resident tabs. The desktop, MCP and CLI preview the actual native paste through the shared renderer. Hover retires provisional sources without creating history. A late native refusal restores the resident cut before the project locks are released. Regression source covers rendered pixels, exact native16 data, disk cleanup after hover and refusal, and history.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-10. A disk-backed native tab can copy or Shift-move editable layers into a resident tab through tab drag-and-drop or the shared project transfer commands. Hover renders the actual destination draft. The source cut uses the native command worker, and a refused final cut restores the resident destination before either project lock is released. Regression source covers rendered pixels, exact 16-bit samples and retained originals, a late reservation and history on both sides. Installed acceptance remains pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-10. Whole-layer clipboard paste now works in both directions between resident and disk-backed native tabs. Native-to-resident paste reads only the copied tree's occupied tiles within resident limits, restores editable definitions and checks the destination revision again before committing. Resident-to-native paste stages the occupied planes in the native transaction. Both paths preserve masks, retained originals and exact 16-bit words, with any promotion in the same undo entry. Regression source covers editable round trips, oversized plane refusal, undo, redo and provisional disk cleanup. Runtime clipboard acceptance remains pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-10. Marquee selection now includes the exact release position, even when it arrives without a final drag-motion event. W/E/R and Free Transform bind drag endpoints to primary-button events and use the Shift state at release. Batched hover movement or pointer departure no longer supplies the release sample. Regression source covers selection bounds and undo at native8/16, selection-only W/E/R preview and commit, and pending Free Transform values. Physical input acceptance remains pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-10. Protected native PSD/PSB opens retain bounded source-layer inspection from the same held index as the saved composite. The protection dialog and MCP/CLI state report expose names, folders, bounds, visibility, mask presence and source tags. Original records remain separate from the one read-only display layer. Regression source covers PSD8 and PSB16 saved samples, folder relationships and state projection. Runtime acceptance remains pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-10. Default-quality native canvas previews retain a bounded full-resolution margin around cropped viewports. The current request remains reusable during small pans, including while it is in flight. Capture still requires current source and epoch proof and complete viewport coverage. The retry after a render refusal uses the exact visible region. Regression source covers native8/16 reuse, viewport-boundary refresh, fallback and the full-resolution sampling limit. Runtime latency and regression execution remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-10. Photoshop RGB8 Curves now accepts sparse channel records and combined master and RGB curves. Omitted records are neutral. Combined curves round each channel to a byte before applying the master, with exact halfway values rounded to even. Direct pixel calibration matched 27,648 channel samples in a sparse fixture and 86,931 eligible channel samples across three combined adjustments. Resident format 59 and native channel marker 2 bind the mapping. Editable import still requires the complete saved composite at the original depth. RGB16 multi-point and combined Photoshop curves remain protected. Regression execution and receiving-editor acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-09. RGB8 PSD/PSB Curves now supports Photoshop's natural cubic spline for three through sixteen points, with a prepared spline shared by the inspector and CPU/GPU lookup paths. Direct inspection matched all 27,648 native channel samples in a saved RGB8 Photoshop fixture. Point editing stays on the standard coordinate grid and format 58 retains the definition. RGB16 Photoshop spline import remains protected after observed native rounding differences. PeerBrush's editable native16 spline settings save through the disclosed raster fallback. Legacy Brightness/Contrast also stays protected after the published model missed 20 distinct byte inputs in a Photoshop fixture. Windows library and test targets passed compile-only checking. Regression execution and receiving-editor acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-09. Standard RGB8/16 PSD/PSB Threshold now preserves editable `thrs` records and original raster and mask channels. The shared effect, adjustment and filter paths use depth-specific luminosity comparisons and preserve alpha. Direct inspection matched 38,636 eligible RGB8 pixels and 13,308 native16 channel samples in Photoshop's saved Threshold fixtures. Resident format 57 binds the new adjustment and protects earlier readers. Regression source covers byte halfway ties, native16 boundaries, protected composite mismatch, clipped standard rows, settings validation and native edit previews. Whole-image Posterize also accepts its existing mapping setting through the filter validator. Windows library and test targets passed compile-only checking. Regression execution and receiving-editor acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-09. Standard PSD/PSB Posterize extends to RGB16. The shared mapper bins and floors palette steps on Photoshop's internal tonal scale, then stores the steps at native16 precision. Independent inspection of the saved Photoshop fixture matched 13,308 original-depth channel samples across four opaque, fully masked panels. Format 56 and native marker 2 identify the calibrated mapping. Earlier Nearest and Legacy bins retain their rendering and save behavior. Regression source covers native bucket edges, palette steps, existing source replay, downgrade refusal, clipped standard rows and exact composite protection. Windows library and test targets passed compile-only checking. Regression execution and receiving-editor acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-09. RGB8 PSD/PSB Posterize now imports and saves standard `post` adjustments with truncated Photoshop bins. Existing effects keep their nearest-level mapping by default. The shared engine applies the selected rule at native depth, and the inspector exposes both mappings. Standard records require the bounded 8-bit adjustment shape. Resident source format 55 and a native Source36 marker retain the mapping when an effect is disabled or saved through raster fallback. Regression source covers exact bucket boundaries, native16 sample handling, clipped standard rows, malformed or mismatched composites, older source refusal and editable reopening. Windows library and test targets passed compile-only checking. Test execution, Photoshop round-trip acceptance and RGB16 Photoshop calibration remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-09. Standard PSD/PSB Curves now retains shifted input points in two-point master or independent RGB records. The graph keeps these records representable while dragging. Resident source format 54 and native Source36 readback verify the resulting definitions, including exact comparison of native channels in Source36 and the original-depth composite gate for conventional imports. Regression source covers valid and malformed records, native8/16 precision, downgrade refusal, and save/readback. The source manifest now lists every file under `src` and `tests`. Windows library and test targets passed compile-only checking. Test execution and receiving-editor acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-09. Native PNG selection files now use the shared held, streamed PNG reader with its 3 GiB encoded limit; inline base64 remains bounded to 64 MiB. Native8/16 input channels, default-image selection semantics, complete chunk checksums, source hashes and final file-change checks remain enforced. Decoded file sources are explicitly retired after coverage sampling, including cancellation or failure. Regression source covers files larger than the resident pool, ancillary-data corruption, held-file write exclusion on Windows and same-size/same-timestamp late changes on Unix. Windows library and test targets passed compile-only checking; tests and native runtime/resource acceptance remain unexecuted.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-09. Native Wand/Quick/Object selections, PNG selection import and selection refinement no longer inherit the resident 16-megapixel canvas cap. The sampled-selection guide retains exact streamed document pixels; flood admission scales with area within the existing hard work bound. Native PNG selection dimensions now reach 16000 pixels per edge and 256 million pixels, with the existing 64 MiB encoded transport limit, complete CRC/hash validation and file-change checks retained. Coverage stays scalar8 while artwork retains its original native depth and source owners. Regression source covers wide-canvas sampled modes, streamed 8/16-bit PNG fixtures above 16 megapixels, file/base64 placement, canceled coverage staging, dense flood above the previous work budget, soft refinement and history. Windows library and test targets passed compile-only checking; tests, runtime memory/latency and native acceptance remain unexecuted. Larger encoded selection-file transport and other per-command limits remain unfinished.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-09. Native selection/path mask creation, mask refinement and color-mask targets no longer inherit the resident 16-megapixel area cap. Existing tile/halo kernels retain native8/16 mask data, source ownership, shared memory/disk admission, stack limits, cancellation and atomic history. Edge guides now share the streamed canvas sampling grid through 16000 pixels per edge, retaining exact document rectangles and one sample per pixel; complete-frame preview callers keep their own cap. Contiguous color-mask predicate admission scales with image area up to the existing hard work limit. Regression source covers full-size masks, local path targets on large canvases, original native words, selection preservation, undo/redo, staged-tile cancellation, full-detail guides above 67,108,864 pixels and dense color matching above the former work budget. Windows library and test targets passed compile-only checking; tests, runtime memory/latency and native acceptance remain unexecuted. Image-based selection/refinement and other per-command limits remain unfinished.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-09. Native geometric selection commands no longer inherit the resident 16-megapixel cap. Rectangle, ellipse, polygon/path selection, feathering, combinations, invert and expand/contract use the existing admitted row/stripe pipeline through the native 16000-pixel edge limit. Shared memory/disk admission, bounded feather/morphology radii, contour complexity, cancellation, reservations and atomic history remain enforced. Regression source covers far-corner soft coverage on 16000-square native8/16 scenes, unchanged artwork ownership, undo/redo, cancellation after a staged tile, and large-canvas combination/morphology membership. Windows library and test targets passed compile-only checking; tests, runtime latency and native acceptance remain unexecuted. Image-based selection/refinement and mask-creation limits remain separate unfinished work.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-09. Levels now supports independent RGB channel controls and shared rendering for layer effects, adjustments and filters. Native channel mappings apply before the master, preserving alpha, hidden samples and native16 precision with budgeted lookup tables. Standard PSD/PSB records support independent RGB settings with a neutral master; combined nonneutral Photoshop master/channel definitions remain protected pending calibration. Resident source format 50 and native semantic marker version 3 protect channel arrays, including disabled effects. Regression source covers scalar validation, exact samples, channel isolation, CPU/GPU lookup offsets, native history, source downgrades, standard record replay and viewing controls without source mutation. Windows library and test targets passed compile-only checking, including the final GPU assertion and catalog update; tests and native/Photoshop acceptance remain unexecuted.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-09. Levels now supports explicit exact input intervals and gamma 0.1–9.99. New effects without supplied settings use exact mapping; existing settings without the marker retain legacy minimum-span rendering. Native8 CPU/GPU lookup construction avoids early sample projection, and native16 uses the same full-precision mapping. Photoshop master import now accepts valid narrow intervals and upper gamma values. Resident source format 49 and native semantic marker version 2 prevent older readers from silently changing the mapping. The gamma handle preserves untouched native endpoints, and exact controls expose finer values. Regression source covers boundaries, legacy pixels, native samples, lookup agreement, pointer input, native history and source roundtrips. Windows library and test targets passed compile-only checking, including the final exact-import correction; tests and native/Photoshop acceptance remain unexecuted.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-09. Master RGB Levels now has standard PSD/PSB import and emission in resident and native save paths. All channel records and optional extended records are validated; unsupported channel changes, ranges and definitions remain protected. Editable import requires an exact full composite match. Save eligibility requires exact integer settings, a supported canvas adjustment and full effect strength; current standard raster/mask data remains present. Resident source format 48 binds emitted Levels settings/blend; native source replay retains historical baked plans. Save review identifies standard adjustments. Regression source covers strict records, native8/16 sources, mismatched composites, forged source bindings, earlier layouts and native row/clipping/mask comparisons. Windows library and test targets passed compile-only checking; tests and native/Photoshop acceptance remain unexecuted.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-09. Levels now has output black/white controls shared by layer effects, adjustment layers and whole-image filters. Both native depths apply the output range after input/gamma mapping and retain alpha and hidden RGB; equal and reversed endpoints are supported. Nondefault settings require resident source format 47, including disabled effects and filters. Native Source36 emits and validates a derived Levels output marker so older strict readers refuse the new semantics. Regression source covers exact native samples, validation, native edit/history comparison, composite rows and PSD/PSB source roundtrips. Photoshop levl import/export is still unsupported. Windows library and test targets passed compile-only checking; tests and native/Photoshop acceptance remain unexecuted.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-09. Clipboard reuse now compares native16 samples when both images supply them. A different native image with identical display bytes keeps its incoming precision and uses normal external-image placement instead of stale retained coordinates. Existing display-only retention and external native16 input beside a retained eight-bit copy keep their behavior. Regression source covers equal/different samples, absent/equal sequence values, retained-copy preservation and undo/redo. Windows library and test targets passed compile-only checking. Tests and installed-platform acceptance remain unexecuted.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-09. Shared native row staging copies contiguous native16 tile-row spans and validates only actual tile padding. Exact native8 projection checks, complete row/tile sequencing, cancellation and source ownership remain enforced. Regression source covers wide row samples, late invalid channel words, mid-row interruption and right/bottom/corner padding; a sparse-result lifetime correction preserves the memory-retirement assertion. Windows library and test targets passed compile-only checking. Tests, runtime performance and native acceptance remain unexecuted.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-09. Native selected paint copy samples occupied source column spans inside each render band, so a narrow painted column no longer consumes work for the full canvas width. Empty selected output remains zero, while full-row native8 color conversion and shared mask/selection math remain unchanged. The fixed-size span index uses source-aligned tile boundaries. Regression source adds tall-column native8/16 duplication on a 16000-square canvas with exact output, opacity/mask, history and cleanup checks. Windows library and test targets passed compile-only checking; tests and runtime measurements remain pending. Full-row storage/conversion and dense/derived limits remain.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-09. Native selected-pixel copy/duplication skips source-empty bands for paint targets without active effects, retaining the shared renderer and full-row color conversion elsewhere. The native row writer records zero runs without scanning full empty stripes and preserves partial output/cancellation rules. Regression source covers sparse 16000-square native8/16 duplication, mask/opacity, exact stored samples, history and cleanup; generated fill/stroke pixels retain the full render path. Windows library and test targets passed compile-only checking. Tests, runtime and performance acceptance remain unexecuted. Dense and derived copy limits remain unchanged.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-09. Clipboard copy/cut preflight now enforces the paste decoder working-memory budget as well as retained transport bytes. Native8/16 oversized refusal precedes scene capture and preserves existing clipboard samples, origin, sequence, source files and history. Bitmap writes validate source buffer shape; macOS/Linux also encode PNG before publication to enforce the later paste-command cap. That platform path adds an encoding pass. Windows library and test targets passed compile-only checking. Test execution and installed-platform acceptance remain pending; the macOS/Linux conditional writer has not been built on those platforms.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-09. Native OS clipboard transport now accepts 16000-pixel edges while retaining the 256 MiB retained-pixel cap, decode/import budgets and 128 MiB PNG cap. Windows PNG/DIB decoding, native16 PNG encoding and paste commands share the wider geometry. Complete PNG validation, explicit animation refusal, color/orientation conversion and pre-clear encoding remain mandatory. Regression source covers wide copy/roundtrip, native samples and hidden RGB, DIB orientation, paste undo/redo, invalid buffers and cleanup. Windows library and test targets passed compile-only checking, including corrected destination-layer and paint-layer lookup fixtures. Tests and installed-platform acceptance have not been run.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-09. Native inline PNG placement/paste now uses native static-image geometry up to 16000 pixels per edge. Encoded transport remains limited to 128 MiB, inline paste retains the cumulative import-byte budget, and animation retains its existing restrictions. Regression source covers wide native8/16 inline operations, precision promotion, rollback and undo/redo, plus wide-animation refusal before staging. Windows library and test targets passed compile-only checking; tests have not been executed. Wide OS clipboard transport and runtime acceptance remain open.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-09. OS-copy preflight now uses the same geometry, precision and 256 MiB retained-byte checks as native bitmap materialization, rejecting known oversized buffers before rendering. Regression source covers early byte-budget refusal and clipboard preservation. Wide native16 PNG patch/place fixtures now encode at their intended native dimensions. Windows library and test targets passed compile-only checking; tests have not been executed. Wide OS clipboard support and runtime acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-09. Native selected-pixel duplication now uses source-backed copy bounds at native canvas dimensions. OS bitmap copy applies its existing resident geometry check before capturing/rendering a scene. Regression source covers wide native8/16 samples with mask/opacity, coordinates, source preservation, cancellation and undo/redo, plus early OS refusal without clipboard mutation. The retained-byte transport refusal fixture now reaches its intended budget guard. Windows library and test targets passed compile-only checking; tests have not been executed. Large-area work limits, wide OS transport and runtime acceptance remain unfinished.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-09. Native image resize skips destination tiles whose bilinear source windows contain no stored tiles. Its work preflight counts the same raster color/mask footprints, while editable definitions and fills retain full-frame accounting. Native output uses admitted tiles and bounded two-row source reads with the existing sampling arithmetic. Regression source covers sparse 16000-square color/mask resizing, exact native8/16 samples, cancellation cleanup and dense-work refusal under the unchanged 128-million-sample limit. Windows library and test targets passed compile-only checking; tests have not been executed. Runtime, dense-path performance and full-size resource acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-09. Native layer merge now admits combined frames up to 16000 pixels per edge through an explicit native policy in the shared geometry validator. Resident merges retain their previous policy. Bounded native rendering, original precision, source admission and guarded publication remain in place. Regression source covers wide/tall frames, exact samples and transparent gaps, sparse output, oversized refusal, cancellation and undo/redo. Windows library and test targets passed compile-only checking; tests have not been executed. Full-size runtime/resource acceptance remains pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-09. Native effect baking, merge, selected-pixel duplication, image resize and image import no longer count shared raster references against the resident 512 MiB document estimate. Physical source ownership and native memory/disk admission remain mandatory, as do decoder, geometry, work and effect limits. Regression source covers original-depth small-edit equivalence beside large shared sources, no-op resize, source preservation and abort/cancellation cleanup. Windows library and test targets passed compile-only checking; tests have not been executed. Dense large-operation and runtime acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-09. Native whole-layer paste no longer applies the resident document's 512 MiB raster estimate to shared source references. Existing physical source admission, metadata reservations and precision-promotion admission govern the operation. Regression source covers native8/16 clipboards wider than resident canvas limits with more than 512 MiB of repeated references under an 8 MiB disk budget, unchanged disk usage, exact channel words and undo/redo after clipboard release. Windows library and test targets passed compile-only checking; tests have not been executed. Runtime and installed clipboard acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-09. Natural-size native image placement reuses one bounded source window per destination tile. Same-depth replacement copies native rows directly while retaining exact change detection, hidden RGB and native16 words. Composition, precision promotion and scaled placement retain their shared arithmetic and admission. Regression source covers translated placement across tile boundaries, native8/16 input combinations, unchanged samples, padding and abort cleanup. Windows library and test targets passed compile-only checking; tests have not been executed. Runtime and latency qualification remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-09. Native image placement skips empty background regions during layer and companion-mask expansion. Verified full aligned tiles can retain their existing physical references; remaining same-depth background regions copy native rows, while former edge tiles are rebuilt to exclude old padding. Procedural fills and precision promotion keep their existing paths. Regression source covers sparse 15999 by 9000 color/mask expansion, retained references, native channel words and hidden RGB, padding exclusion, bounded work and cancellation cleanup. Windows compile-only and whitespace checks passed; tests have not been executed. Native runtime, latency and release acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-09. Native static PNG placement, file paste and raw patches now share the existing 16000-pixel/3 GiB native file admission. Shared placement geometry takes an explicit resident/native canvas policy, so native destination and combined layer frames can reach 16000 pixels while resident limits remain intact. Verified static PNG file paste retains admitted native tiles instead of applying the resident clipboard-buffer estimate. Regression source covers far-corner patches, wide new/existing-layer placement, scaling, file paste, native channel promotion and hidden RGB, undo/redo, cancellation cleanup and atomic rollback after an oversized frame. Windows compile-only checking passed; tests have not been executed. Animation, inline PNG and other-codec decode limits, full-size runtime/resource acceptance and the wider backlog remain open.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-09. Streamed canvas conversion delivers each tile immediately to the upload batch instead of retaining a complete horizontal band of converted images. The batch can wait for actual image retirement before allocating the next tile. Native8/16 regression source covers an 8192-pixel-wide preview whose source band and duplicate upload images cannot fit together under an 8 MiB resident budget, plus cancellation with a renderer-held image. Windows compile-only checking passed; tests have not been executed. Source-band admission, actual GPU/whole-process memory and responsiveness acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-09. Native raster AI fades can now pair shared-engine stripe renders when the observed before-frame has no retained CPU baseline, including the full 16000-square display grid. A bounded queue and joined helper preserve the existing alpha arithmetic, admission, cancellation and derived-source cleanup. Published receipts carry the original display tag across steps without retaining source sessions or endpoint pixels. Regression source covers native8/16 parity, multi-stripe cancellation, full-size partial streaming and publication continuity. Windows compile-only checking passed; tests have not been executed. Rendering both snapshots per step adds work, and whole-process memory, GPU capacity, latency and native acceptance remain unmeasured. The wider backlog remains open.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-09. PSD/PSB saves now include bounded off-frame samples in the standard channel for unlinked masks with one integer-translated retained paint step and an optional constant fill. Both writers preserve the current mask inside the layer frame and use the retained source outside it. Resident format 39 binds the expanded record to the editable source. Source36 verifies the expanded layout and replays older mask layouts exactly. Regression source covers standard-only reopen at both depths, the saved composite and all three Source36 layouts. Runtime and Photoshop acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-09. Bounded off-frame samples in supported unlinked Photoshop masks now open as retained native mask originals. The visible mask is projected onto the original layer frame. A later Color move can reveal the original samples without moving the mask in document coordinates. Imports that exceed retained-source admission use the protected saved composite. PSD/PSB saves preserve the full original in PeerBrush editable sources and emit the current standard mask frame. Regression source covers native8/16, all four channel encodings, reopen and a later independent move. Runtime qualification remains pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-09. Supported standard PSD/PSB raster masks now retain their link state on open and save. The mask rectangle stays in document coordinates. Unlinked output writes an explicit neutral layer-mask effects mode. Unsupported compositing modes and unlinked mask samples outside the layer frame remain protected. Resident format 38 binds the link record to the editable source. Source36 compares the new standard record and retains an exact legacy replay path. Native8/16 regression source covers both formats, older saves, native mask samples and conflicting link data. Photoshop receiving-editor acceptance remains pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-09. Free Transform picks the nearest Grid Warp node where hit targets overlap. Under Distort or Perspective, an off-center drag moves the node by the pointer’s document-space displacement. Snap applies to the node and Shift keeps its other axis fixed. Regression source covers overlapping targets, the projected drag and preservation of neighboring nodes. Native pointer and tablet acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-09. Native raster row reads copy contiguous spans from pinned tiles. Missing tiles and pixels outside the logical frame remain transparent, including where a physical edge tile contains padding. Frame expansion and the reframed Smudge baseline now copy native rows through the same reader. Regression source covers native8/16 bytes across sparse tiles, hidden RGB, edge padding, shifted frame placement and invalid read windows. Runtime and latency qualification remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-09. On an unchanged layer frame, native Smudge reads the requested working tile or strip directly from staged replacements and the captured source. Explicit zero replacements hide the original tile until restored. Read footprints retain their admitted native pins and source checks. The complete candidate index is assembled at finalization. Native8/16 regression source covers a strip spanning changed, erased and untouched tiles, cancellation and restoration. Runtime and latency qualification remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-09. Native Smudge applies final soft-selection coverage only to tiles staged by the ordered stroke. Unchanged tiles in an expanded frame skip baseline and selection sampling. Coverage still applies once against the captured original pixels after the last dab. Native8/16 regression source covers a far-corner stroke through half coverage on a 16000 by 9000 canvas. Runtime and latency qualification remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-09. Native layer expansion uses the paint and retouch tile footprints to skip empty, untouched regions. Frame remaps for companion planes and smudge also skip empty regions. Verified full aligned tiles outside the edit can retain their backing references, while former edge tiles are rebuilt so padding stays outside the artwork. Procedural fill and primitive staging retain their full-frame edit scope. Native8/16 regression source covers a far-corner stroke on a 16000 by 9000 canvas, retained source words and exclusion of old padding. Runtime qualification remains pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-09. Native source inventory checks read file identity and hard-link count together for each held and named file. Checks still refuse aliases, replaced names, extra links and changed sizes or modification times. Unix identity opens also reuse the regular-file check on the no-follow, nonblocking handle. This reduces metadata queries during guarded previews and other native source operations. Regression source covers extra links, replaced names and changed bytes. Native execution and latency measurements remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-09. Native paint, erase, clone and heal use a bounded tile index to skip groups of dabs outside each tile. Coverage still uses the original dab order, shared brush math and native channel precision. The regression source covers a stroke that leaves a tile and returns to it after crossing several other tiles. Live preview latency and tablet acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-09. Frame-changing selected native pixel transforms visit occupied source regions and the paste destination. Full aligned tiles outside the cut and paste can retain their physical references. Former edge tiles are rebuilt so padding stays outside logical content. Linked-mask shifts and reframe reservation checks skip regions with no physical tiles. Procedural fill materialization retains its full-frame admission. A native8/16 regression source covers a one-pixel expansion of a 15999 by 9000 layer and an untouched tile reference. Runtime qualification remains pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-09. Selected native pixel transforms that retain the layer frame now process the cut and destination regions and reuse untouched backing tiles. The same operation budget checks sampling, with full-frame admission for materialized fills and provisional planes that cannot reuse their backing. Cut coverage, paste sampling, and reservations remain bound to the original document coordinates. The large-canvas regression source checks a one-pixel move and retention of an untouched physical tile. Runtime qualification remains pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-09. Native whole-layer mirrors and quarter-turns with integer placement plan their output from occupied source tiles. They preflight the actual tile sampling work and preserve original channel words, including RGB under transparent alpha. Selected transforms and merged retouch sampling use native geometry admission with their existing work and footprint bounds. Source regression coverage includes a 16000-square sparse mirror and a native16 quarter-turn with hidden RGB. Runtime and platform qualification remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-09. Native Photoshop Save and Open use the same bounded full-layer effect preparation as canvas rendering and export. Standard layer and merged samples are checked against the prepared effect output. Original raster and mask samples remain separate from derived effect pixels. Conventional Photoshop adoption still requires an exact saved-composite match. Effect staging is retired before publication. Native8/16 drop-shadow round-trip regression source checks the visible shadow, editable effect, and original samples. Runtime and cross-platform qualification remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-09. Native New Canvas, canvas geometry, and document settings use a 16000-pixel edge limit in workspaces with native source service. Source36 Save and Open share the same large-canvas Photoshop index profile. Empty rows in plain paint composites skip per-pixel rendering while retaining the standard white matte and alpha. Resident canvas limits and conventional Photoshop decode limits still apply to their existing paths. The regression source covers a far-corner edit on a 16000-square canvas and native8/16 PSB reopen across the former edge limit. Full-size runtime and platform qualification remain pending.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-09. File Export and the document export command now render from admitted native scenes for PNG, WebP and JPEG XL. PNG and JPEG XL retain native channel depth. Lossless WebP projects the rendered output to 8 bits and leaves the working document intact. The native export writer verifies staged bytes, retains the previous destination on confirmed replacement, and checks project authority at publication. The native output cap is 4 GiB. The production export panel and protocol also plan and publish native batches with selected roots, rendered alpha bounds, padding, and scaled variants. PNG and lossless WebP previews sample the planned output grid at bounded display size. JPEG XL previews use the actual encoded result within the current display decoder limits. Full-size canvas runtime qualification remains open.

Unreleased development update, 2026-11-09. Native code requests now use the shared start, status and cancel routing. Jobs retain admitted metadata and PNG receipts, and the status record preserves a successful edit when feedback is interrupted. In a workspace with a configured source service, the New Canvas dialog and projects.new create a native canvas from either kind of tab. The document protocol can also create a native canvas in the existing tab. Protocol replies are admitted before switching, and in-place New rechecks the current source and active reservations at publication. In-place Open on native projects can now reopen PeerBrush native PSD and PSB saves. It checks the held file and compares standard layer channels and the merged composite before installing the qualified source. File Open, queued file activation and projects.open also reopen these saves in independent native tabs when the source service is configured. The native reader recognizes saves with no tile chunks. PNG, JPEG, JPEG XL, BMP, Netpbm, QOI, TGA, GIF, WebP, TIFF and ICO can open as native images through File Open, the project protocol and in-place Open on native tabs. The chooser inspects verified bytes and uses the same held file for the selected animation frame, TIFF page or ICO variant. Protocol callers supply the corresponding index when a choice is required. SVG and SVGZ open through the same routes and retain their editable markup with the rendered layer. Opened images start unsaved with no working destination. The existing image decoder limits still apply. Conventional PSD and PSB can stream supported layer and mask channels at their original depth into provisional native tiles without building resident layer rasters. The shared Photoshop parser checks metadata and the native renderer must match the saved composite exactly before File Open, projects.open or in-place Open adopts the tiles. Inputs that need compatibility decoding retain the previous depth and editability checks. The conventional Photoshop decode limits still apply. Runtime qualification and macOS worker protection remain outstanding.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-08: the internal native procedural path now returns an admitted completion receipt with optional actual PNG feedback. Completion headers are reserved before publication. The shared edit reply retains its non-serialized candidate generation key; feedback captures only that committed native generation, then renders and encodes through the shared preview engine with source-session/service checks off Engine and workspace locks. A changed or nominally replaced document, cancellation, quota refusal or feedback callback panic preserves the applied receipt and returns a feedback warning instead of reporting a discarded edit. Encoded PNG chunks are shared across owned response handoffs, while each metadata projection receives its own source-pool admission. Response slots retain actual bytes and document coordinates through ordinary JSON and MCP image framing after the completion owner is dropped. No source session enters the retained completion. Regression source covers native8/native16 pixel parity, repeated response lifetimes, both client formats, generation binding, human changes, lost services, early/late interruption, callback panic, capacity refusal and detached/foreign response rejection. Windows compile-only checking passed across 109 application targets, with warnings remaining; tests have not been executed. Native job storage and public start/status routing remain unfinished, so public code still requires resident authority. macOS limits, native runtime/platform acceptance, New/Open adoption, 16000-square qualification and the wider backlog remain open.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-08: the internal native procedural path now connects supervised command generation to the shared native edit transaction. The code job retains its exact source, active owned task, description and deadline through capture, preparation and the final memory-only publication fence. Declared-scope containment is shared by resident/native callers and checked against each evolving native draft, both before its command handler and against actual prepared output; refusal retires current and earlier provisional outputs off-lock. The ordinary history path retains actor/task attribution and a single transaction. Confirmed child exits now detach their registry owner immediately, avoiding a delayed large-ticket refund before native preparation, while uncertain exits remain retained. Regression source covers native8/native16 channels and hidden RGB, undo/redo, later-step rollback, selection-driven scope changes, cancellation, takeover, lost services, nominal same-ID/revision source replacement, locks, deadlines and preservation of human edits. Windows compile-only checking passed across 109 application targets, with warnings remaining; tests have not been executed. Public native start/status routing and owned PNG feedback remain unfinished, so public code still requires resident authority. macOS limits, native runtime/platform acceptance, New/Open adoption, 16000-square qualification and the wider backlog remain open.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-08: native procedural command generation now has a real parent process supervisor. It captures the actual native source, reserves child capacity plus the explicit pump stack/header envelope from that source pool, launches the current application worker with a cleared environment, and requires both complete framed transport/EOF and a successful actual child exit before returning admitted commands. The parent retains Windows kill-on-close job ownership; Windows and Linux child-local limits still precede script input, while other platforms refuse native execution. Cancellable pipes are now shared with JPEG XL. Watchdog cancellation, timeout and panic interrupt pipe I/O independently of process death; callback/source owners stay in joined scopes. A fixed owner registry and live reaper retain the actual Child, job and memory charge after an uncertain stop, releasing capacity only after observing exit. No document source/session is handed to the child. Regression source covers actual child success/failure, silent-child timeout, blocked pipes, watchdog panic, pre-launch refusal and retained-owner capacity. Windows compile-only checking passed across 109 application targets, including JPEG XL callers, with warnings remaining; tests have not been executed. Native engine preparation/publication and owned status/PNG feedback remain unfinished; public code remains resident-only. macOS resource enforcement, native runtime/whole-memory/platform acceptance, New/Open adoption, 16000-square qualification and the wider backlog remain open.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-08: the native procedural parent exchange now serves frozen source pixel/patch reads over the bounded protocol and retains returned commands with admission from the actual source pool. Shared command validation preserves the interpreter editing surface; parent validation additionally checks exact native pixel schemas, decoded payload lengths/depth and cumulative replacement pixels without trusting child read requests. Command JSON is scanned before DOM admission and strictly parsed for unique keys/canonical framing. Task/source checks surround reads, replies, command acknowledgement and terminal EOF. Failed reads produce explicit errors, never invented zero pixels; no returned command changes live state. Borrowed document metadata serialization is shared with the interpreter. Regression source covers native8/native16 channel parity, retained ownership, failed reads, forbidden/malformed commands, aggregate forged patches, task takeover, cancellation during acknowledgement, admission refusal and trailing output. Windows compile-only checking passed across 109 application targets, with warnings remaining; tests have not been executed. Actual parent process supervision, successful child exit, native command preparation/publication and owned status/PNG feedback remain unfinished; public code remains resident-only. Native runtime/platform acceptance, New/Open adoption, 16000-square qualification and the wider backlog remain open.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-08: the procedural worker now has a child entry point that runs before preferences and UI setup. The child receives bounded metadata and native8/native16 pixel replies, evaluates through the shared interpreter, and returns commands without opening document sources or publishing edits. Windows job limits and Linux resource limits are applied before reading script input; unsupported platforms refuse execution. Regression source covers native channel replies, returned commands, invalid metadata/build labels, terminal interpreter failures and parent aborts that cannot be caught to emit commands. Windows compile-only checking passed across 109 application targets, with warnings remaining; tests have not been executed. Parent process supervision, native source brokering, publication and owned status/PNG feedback remain unfinished; public code remains resident-only. macOS resource enforcement, native runtime acceptance and the wider backlog remain open.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-08: native procedural worker transport now has an internal ordered half-duplex state machine and bounded frame codec. Start metadata/script sizes, document coordinates, patch totals, progress order, command count/bytes and reply shapes are checked before accepting payloads. Frames bind a nonzero exchange identity, exact sequence and control/body digest; body storage is caller-provided and must already be admitted. Native8 replies reject high channel bytes, while native16 retains full little-endian words. Partial/ambiguous reads, writes, flushes or late cancellation poison the session rather than allow replay. Parent transport completion requires the exact command terminal state and EOF; actual successful child exit remains an independent mandatory publication check. Abort and recoverable read errors have distinct states. None of these counters/hashes or metadata grants source, task, executable or filesystem authority. Regression source covers native8/16 exchanges, pixel budgets, terminal/EOF ordering, wrong turns, replay, partial I/O, corruption/truncation, valid-checksum malformed schema/depth, caller-storage refusal and start bounds. Windows compile-only checking passed across 109 application targets, with warnings remaining; tests have not been executed. This is internal codec preparation. Process launch/resource enforcement, evaluator/broker integration, native publication and owned status/PNG feedback remain unfinished; public code remains resident-only. New/Open adoption, 16000-square/runtime/latency/platform/release qualification and the wider backlog remain open.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-08: the shared procedural evaluator now consumes dimensions/channel depth, script metadata, raw pixel-read methods and a progress callback instead of owning a document. The existing public resident path uses its frozen reader with the same scope checks; source preparation and final publication remain separate. Script metadata is serialized directly from borrowed resident/native document views into Rhai, removing the intermediate JSON value and owned layer-metadata vector. The interpreter validates dimensions/script bounds and rejects incomplete patch replies or native16 words presented as native8, rather than truncating channels or inventing pixels. Resident raw-read coordinate translation now uses wide checked coordinates before accessing original samples. Regression source preserves the prior frozen-read/channel/atomic behavior and adds metadata parity, metadata-only evaluator execution at both depths, malformed reply length/depth and source-error refusal. Windows compile-only checking passed across 109 application targets, with warnings remaining; tests have not been executed. This input boundary grants no source or editing authority. Worker process launch/resource ownership, bounded framed transport, native command publication and owned status/PNG feedback remain unfinished; public code still requires resident authority. Ordinary New/Open adoption, 16000-square/runtime/latency/platform/release qualification and the wider backlog remain open.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-08: procedural evaluation is now separate from command preparation/publication. The interpreter and scope drain before an actual unique context unwrap moves the completed command allocation; a retained callback/context, open pixel buffer or empty command list cannot proceed. The previous deep command-buffer clone is removed. Command byte checks stream borrowed serialization instead of allocating encoded JSON, and Rhai edit maps pass that check through their existing Dynamic serializer before JSON conversion. Rejected oversized/count-limited appends leave the valid buffer and byte ledger unchanged; actual normalized output is checked again before append. Existing source/task/cancellation checks, limits, allowed operations and atomic resident publication remain. Regression source covers native-channel 8/16 frozen reads and emitted patch words, unchanged source/history during evaluation, moved payload ownership, retained-context refusal, escaped-byte/count rejection, unfinished patches, script errors and cancellation. Windows compile-only checking passed across 109 application targets, with warnings remaining; tests have not been executed. Rhai container/operation/time limits do not establish complete allocation admission, including map-key and captured-value storage. The public code route remains resident-only. Native interpreter allocation ownership, command publication, owned status/PNG feedback, New/Open adoption, 16000-square/runtime/latency/platform/release qualification and the wider backlog remain unfinished.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-08: procedural-code source guards now accept the shared document view and compare native authority by its exact generation as well as document/revision. A new internal native reader captures the actual scene and read admission off Engine/workspace locks, verifies project/task/reservations and the configured SourceRuntime, and reads raw paint-layer channels through the existing admitted input tile. One tile is retained; native8 values remain 0-255 and native16 preserves original words and hidden RGB. Bounded patch reads admit their owned word buffer before allocation, enforce declared scopes, check rows and final state, and traverse physical tiles without row-wise cache thrashing. Only translated source exterior supplies known zero; cancellation, task takeover, changed source/service and admission failures return errors. Read buffers retain their own admission after the source reader drops. Regression source compares native8/16 words against the existing resident code reader across offsets/layers/tile boundaries and covers cached-read invalidation, same-revision source replacement, quota refusal, patch ownership/scopes and cancellation. Windows compile-only checking passed across 109 application targets, with warnings remaining; tests have not been executed. Native interpreter admission, command publication, owned status/PNG feedback, ordinary New/Open adoption, 16000-square/runtime/latency/platform/release qualification and the wider backlog remain unfinished. The public code route still requires resident authority; this internal source preparation is unreleased.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-08: native tab registration now shares off-lock metadata/identity preparation and a final memory-only registry update between the existing recovery caller and a new internal blank-tab producer. Recovery still verifies actual checkpoint seals and rechecks its marker before publication; it now requires the exact configured SourceRuntime rather than matching pools alone. The prepared owner stays outside the registry lock on refusal. Shared libraries, chronological history capacity, recovered-unsaved state, project limits, fresh runtime identity and conditional activation retain their existing behavior. Known-zero blank preparation uses actual memory-only read admission and its instance publication fence, preserving source-namespace absence until real source writes. Regression source covers native8/16 transparent rendering/depth, unchanged existing artwork/history, one-use loading controls, conditional activation, missing/replaced service, cancellation, exit, contention, duplicate identity and late capacity refusal. Windows compile-only checking passed across 109 application targets, with warnings remaining; tests have not been executed. This is internal preparation, not ordinary New/Open adoption. Existing editable geometry validation remains unchanged; 16000-square editing is not qualified. Native procedural-code migration, New/Open routing, runtime/latency/platform/release acceptance and the wider backlog remain unfinished.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-08: native proposal review now renders the actual stored candidate on the existing bounded canvas worker and projects display-only uploads through the normal presenter. Original/proposed switching uses separate request identities; candidate pixels never enter committed-frame caches or satisfy committed capture receipts. Admitted UI tags retain a pending-record proof rather than draft/source sessions, and publication, paint, selection feedback and acceptance check that proof so rejection, expiry and record removal invalidate feedback without a base-revision change. Human acceptance uses the existing cancellable edit worker and shared stored-candidate publication path, retaining original AI attribution and one history entry. Refusal preserves the candidate, refreshes its preview and requires another human acceptance; closing cancels queued display work. Regression source covers native8/16 uploaded pixels, view switching, capture exclusion, worker acceptance, terminal invalidation and reservation refusal followed by retry. Windows compile-only checking passed across 109 distinct application targets, with warnings remaining; tests and native runtime qualification have not been executed. Ordinary native adoption, runtime/latency/platform/release acceptance and the wider backlog remain unfinished.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-08: the owned proposal protocol now routes native create/list/preview/accept/reject through the bounded workspace candidate store. Rendered proposal PNGs retain admitted response owners and include document coordinates, source revision and preview-only identity. Detached Value transport refuses before mutation. Acceptance uses the stored human approval path; feedback observes the committed revision, and unavailable post-commit feedback produces an applied-with-warning result. Native edit, transient preview and proposal capture now freeze the existing filter/brush preset semantics before native scope capture, admitting borrowed settings before command copies and preserving explicit overrides. Builtin library lookup borrows cached curated originals instead of cloning the entire library. Regression source covers native8/16 rendered protocol parity, unchanged pre-accept source/history, original actor attribution, detached/invalid/stale/repeated requests, rejection and preset snapshot/cancellation ownership. Windows compile-only checking passed across 109 distinct application targets, with warnings remaining; tests have not been executed. Native proposal UI presentation/acceptance, ordinary native adoption, runtime/latency/platform/release qualification and the wider backlog remain unfinished.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-08: native proposals now have bounded workspace-owned storage; Engine records retain admitted metadata and an atomic binding rather than source sessions. Rejection, expiry, takeover, source changes and record removal invalidate that binding without source I/O under Engine/workspace locks. Shared review/acceptance use the actual stored owner, and the final Engine publication fence rechecks its pending record before marking acceptance. Busy readers and refused retirement retain their real owners and quota; preparing and cleanup slots count toward the four-per-project limit. The existing joined recovery worker performs periodic cleanup and drains retained proposal sessions before instance shutdown. Idle activity requires unique session/root-operation ownership and retains actual instance admission; render, approval and retirement reactivate activity accounting. Teardown performs no implicit verification/unlink, and unresolved files remain in actual custody/startup census. Regression source covers native8/16 state/review/acceptance, terminal routes, reader/debt limits, replaced/expired approval records, real instance close/drain and parked admission with child owners. Windows compile-only checking passed across 109 application targets, with warnings remaining; tests have not been executed. Public protocol/UI wiring, ordinary native adoption, native runtime/latency/platform/release acceptance and the wider backlog remain unfinished.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-08: native proposal candidates now expose shared human acceptance through the ordinary native Engine/history publication fence and edit cancellation boundary. Actor, task and label remain the proposing contributor's; approval does not replay commands or reopen imported files. Current project/source, configured service, task lifetime and reservations are checked before publication. Prepared native history retains its original observation until publication succeeds, so preparation and final-fence refusals return the same frozen candidate and provisional source owner for review, retry or explicit retirement. Successful publication returns an admitted edit response and preserves the existing applied-with-cleanup-warning behavior. Regression source covers native8/16 one-entry undo/redo, original channel words and hidden RGB, changed import files, admission refusal, takeover/cancellation, new reservations and stale AI publication state followed by retry. Windows compile-only checking passed across 109 application targets, with warnings remaining; tests have not been executed. This is an internal acceptance primitive. Live native proposal storage, expiry/rejection draining, the final stored-record approval guard, UI/MCP integration, ordinary native adoption, runtime/platform/release acceptance and the wider backlog remain unfinished.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-08: native proposal preparation now captures the actual shared Engine source, normalized command descriptor, reservations and configured source service. It revalidates the source, task, service and reservations after candidate and attribution preparation. Late refusal retains the prepared owner/session for explicit off-lock retirement and retry. Shared review renders bounded regions, layer/mask targets and selection views; source/task changes during rendering or a replaced source service refuse feedback without consuming the draft. Resident and native proposal preparation share a streaming 100-command/8 MiB wire check without a second encoded payload. Regression source covers native8/16 preview parity, unchanged sources/history, cancellation, takeover, source/service replacement, late refusal and cleanup retry, plus escaped-byte bounds. Windows compile-only checking passed across 109 application targets, with warnings remaining; tests have not been executed. These are internal preparation/review primitives. Live native proposal storage, expiry/rejection draining, shared acceptance, UI/MCP integration, ordinary native adoption, runtime/platform/release acceptance and the wider backlog remain unfinished.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-08: foreground source admission can now interrupt a collection scan and wait for its real exclusive-access guard to leave, instead of losing the request to that maintenance fence. Queued readers acquire their actual read permits before another collection can start. Same-thread probes and unrelated exclusive operations keep their nonblocking refusal. Collection polls interruption during record/root checks; after unlink begins, that one retirement finishes its handle closure, receipt and bookkeeping before yielding. A yield alone does not taint storage. Source-session admission now serializes complete inventory validation so readers waking together avoid transient admission contention; existing session checks retain nonblocking busy handling. Recovery pruning defers rejected source cleanup after revalidating cancellation and actual service trust, allowing a valid native checkpoint while retaining the refused source and quota. Uncertain mutation still blocks fresh admission. Regression source covers reader handoff, reentrant refusal, warm/startup scan interruption, post-unlink completion, concurrent admissions and native8/16 checkpoints beside a poisoned orphan. Windows compile-only checking passed across 109 application targets, with warnings remaining; tests have not been executed. Native input/latency acceptance, Unix retirement, namespace crash durability, ordinary native adoption, runtime/platform/release acceptance and the wider backlog remain unfinished.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-08: Windows source collection now inspects unselected startup files through their actual inventoried observation and an exclusively locked data handle. Complete header, record/hash, tile-index and manifest-chain checks cover the whole held file under the checkpoint/save-journal root census. Inspection never exports a Store/Snapshot, registers a selected prefix, infers an editable newest version or writes source bytes. Refusal restores the exact held observation and quota; successful retirement closes that additional observer before the nominal receipt can settle its logical file. Active owners from an earlier runtime prevent inspection through their retained handle/OS lock. Regression source covers native8/16 empty, tile-only, multiple-commit and tile-tail files; cancellation, scratch admission, corrupt/partial/unknown records; live old owners; unregistered recovery roots; and warm/startup late-fault retention. Windows compile-only checking passed across 109 application targets, with warnings remaining; tests have not been executed. Unix retirement, namespace crash durability, collection latency/human-input acceptance, ordinary native adoption, runtime/platform/release acceptance and the wider backlog remain unfinished.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-08: native Windows recovery maintenance now collects only backing sources held in actual parked custody after their last logical owner disappears. A consumed source session obtains exclusive namespace admission; other readers and retained child permits defer collection. Every candidate requires complete retained-record/index verification, matching physical owners and disk pools, and the full checkpoint/save-journal root census. File and empty-directory retirement use bound DELETE handles; source links must be zero after the DELETE handle closes, and all known source/directory handles must close before namespace/absence checks and a nominal receipt can settle only that logical file reservation. Windows currently proves runtime removal; the shared parent-sync helper is a no-op there, so namespace crash durability remains unqualified. Live scenes/history/clipboard/preview surfaces and registered recovery snapshots retain their actual owners. Pre-attempt refusal restores custody without modifying bytes. Uncertain unlink, directory or settlement outcomes retain debt and block fresh source admission, including panic unwinding. Recovery pruning invokes collection outside Engine/workspace locks. Regression source covers native8/16 committed and tile-only sources, live owners, readers/children, cancellation, admission refusal, foreign/unknown contents, recovery-root preservation, late faults and unchanged startup files. Unopened startup files and Unix platforms remain retained pending independent physical retirement support. Windows compile-only checking passed across 109 application targets, with warnings remaining; tests have not been executed. Native/runtime/platform/release acceptance, ordinary native adoption and the wider backlog remain unfinished.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-08: parked backing custody now supports an exclusive nominal claim that restores the exact source on drop without I/O. Claiming requires the actual parked backing, not an expired weak count. Complete source verification checks every record, manifest chain and tile index against the retained head/read boundary; it never adopts an unconfirmed readonly prefix. Reopened writable snapshots and verification now follow their actual current publication head after later commits, while readonly snapshots remain bounded by explicit read selections. Collection validation admits its bounded streaming working set before backing reads. The checkpoint-root census also verifies complete registered external-save journals through held-file identities and full SHA readback, checks every registered journal member is present, and compares journal proofs across namespace observations. Unknown, partial, replaced, cancelled or changed journals retain all sources. Regression source covers live-root refusal, cancelled claims, writable tile tails, explicit readonly index extension, same-stamp corruption, scratch exhaustion, valid journal census and incomplete closing receipts. Source retirement, handle-bound unlink, quota settlement and automatic collection remain unfinished; parked resources stay charged. Windows compile-only checking passed across 109 application targets, with warnings remaining; tests have not been executed. Native/runtime/platform/release acceptance, ordinary native adoption and the wider backlog remain unfinished.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-08: registered backing stores now transfer their exact held files, immutable identity and admitted read/write index into a charged custodian when their last logical owner drops. That transfer performs no backing I/O. An admitted source session resumes the same known state for lineage retries or explicit checkpoint selection, preserving readonly policy and the original publication head; it does not reopen the file or infer a newest commit. Weak upgrade still reports logical liveness only. Unregistered component stores retain their existing close policy, and a successful unique fragment abort leaves no resumable backing. Regression source covers native8/16 bytes, readonly later-prefix indexes, repeated mirror reuse without file/checkpoint growth, concurrent resumption, admission refusal, completed abort and foreign replacement. Cold-tail fixtures now close the actual runtime before reopening; preservation reads use owned handles while locks remain live. Shared-source deletion and quota refunds remain disabled: parked index/handle admission stays retained until verified collection or runtime teardown. Windows compile-only checking passed across 109 application targets, with warnings remaining; tests have not been executed. Runtime/platform/release acceptance, ordinary native adoption and the wider backlog remain unfinished.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-08: native recovery and provisional outputs can now combine physical tile references from different readonly checkpoint selections. The shared sealing path copies complete required tiles into an owned writable mirror only when no registered selection covers the whole set. Original depth, channel bytes, hidden RGB and tile coordinates remain intact; recovery binds the actual copied owners without changing its format. Captured documents, history and original source publication heads remain unchanged. Regression source covers native8/16 mixed tiles within one surface and across layers, recovery round-trips, output sealing, mirror reuse, alias/foreign-admission refusal and cancellation after mirror admission. Persistent copies retained after failed preparation stay charged; verified shared-source collection is still required. Windows compile-only checking passed across 109 application targets, with warnings remaining; tests have not been executed. Runtime/platform/release acceptance, Unix retention, ordinary native adoption and the wider backlog remain unfinished.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-08: held source owners can now validate explicit later recovery prefixes into an admitted read index after reopening an older prefix. Full record hashes, manifest chains, the previously validated boundary and existing tile identities must agree before the index changes. The original owner, publication head and readonly policy remain intact; cancellation, corruption, forged selectors and memory refusal retain the earlier index. Source checks also revalidate the file header. Readonly re-checkpointing can reuse a fully registered selection that covers every requested reference, without appending or combining partial selections. Regression source covers native8/16 old/middle/new samples, atomic refusal, same-stamp header/payload changes, real shared-file catalog reopening and middle-version re-checkpoint/restore. Preservation fixtures now read owned handles to respect Windows locks. Windows compile-only checking passed across 109 application targets, with warnings remaining; tests have not been executed. Readonly reference unions still require a private native re-seal. Source collection, Unix retention, ordinary native adoption, runtime/platform/release acceptance and the wider backlog remain unfinished.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-08: the periodic worker now publishes native Windows recovery checkpoints at the original 8/16-bit depth, with three-version retention and admitted opaque bookkeeping. Unchanged generations avoid rewrites; pre-publication changes defer work, while edits after durable publication remain dirty for the next tick. Failed pruning records the durable checkpoint and retries without accumulating new versions. The new checkpoint is protected against older future timestamps. Saved/closed project cleanup uses the same bound source service and leaves shared pixel stores charged. PSD and native marker metadata share recovery project/byte limits; source pixels keep their separate disk admission. Regression source covers both depths, history/source preservation, deduplication, real periodic scheduling/join/restore, save/close cleanup, busy retries, clock rollback, changed-source timing, service replacement, unreadable records and quota arithmetic. Windows compile-only checking passed for 109 application targets, with warnings remaining; tests have not been executed. Unreleased source update, 2026-11-08: native checkpoint staging now tracks the exact acknowledged write prefix. Producer errors, short/oversized output, cancellation and SHA refusal can retire an unpublished Windows marker only after complete prefix readback, no-replace detachment, handle-bound unlink, zero remaining links, closure and parent sync. A nominal receipt settles only that bound marker reservation. Modified or unacknowledged bytes, aliases, replaced names, another session, failed creation and uncertain publication retain files/admission rather than guessing cleanup. An unsettled publication marks discovery uncertain, including unwinding after the canonical marker appears, so it cannot report an empty catalog. Inventory capacity is checked before file creation. Existing canonical checkpoints and source pixels remain intact; capture failures still latch. Regression source covers these boundaries and updates cancellation/census expectations. Windows compile-only checking passed across 109 application targets; tests remain unexecuted. Unix cleanup, native autosave publication/retention, shared-source GC, ordinary native adoption, runtime/platform/release acceptance and the wider backlog remain unfinished.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-08: explicit native checkpoint discard now has a Windows handle-bound removal path shared by UI, MCP and CLI. It consumes exclusive source-session admission, validates the actual complete marker and selected sources, and settles only the removed marker charge after unlink, known-handle closure and parent sync. Other checkpoints, open tabs and shared pixel stores retain their owners; pixel files remain charged. Pre-move cancellation/unwinding restores registration; uncertain removal retains roots/debt and requires restart. The chooser confirms disposal, runs it off the UI thread, prevents duplicates and respects Later during refresh. Unix native discard remains disabled pending deletion bound to the held owner. Regression source covers both depths, shared sources, cursor/child refusal, cancellation, interrupted removal, reopening, human-only MCP and chooser confirmation. Windows compile-only checking passed across 109 application targets; tests have not been executed. Source GC, autosave publication, ordinary native adoption, runtime/platform/release acceptance and the wider backlog remain unfinished.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-08: native recovery discovery now reads actual authenticated checkpoint records and selected source prefixes, with admitted bounded display metadata and explicit unreadable-record/service errors. The recovery chooser scans in a background worker, serializes refreshes, preserves a human dismissal when results arrive late and restores listed native checkpoints through the existing guarded path. MCP recovery listing retains legacy versions and adds owned native_versions replies with native:UUID identifiers, nullable unknown metadata and bounded display_name values; restoration revalidates actual sources. Regression source covers native8/16 listings and restored samples, mixed-format UUID collisions, damaged records, cancellation/admission refusal, namespace absence, owned protocol replies and real chooser clicks. Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: explicit recovery restoration now accepts fully decoded native backing checkpoints through the configured authenticated source service. It validates editable metadata without a resident pixel copy, assigns fresh runtime identities, retains exact source owners and registers a new unsaved native tab under the actual instance/workspace fence. Marker SHA is re-read against its validated registry record before insertion. Read-only/profiled documents, unsupported layer/effect/mask/source definitions, invalid hierarchy/selections/transforms and exhausted revisions are refused with recovery retained. Existing-only source admission prevents missing recovery requests from creating a source namespace. Regression source covers both channel depths, exact metadata/pixels, independent restored tabs, activation, real source-inventory reopening, cancellation, foreign sessions/services, tab limits and changed markers. Platform/runtime/release acceptance and the wider backlog remain unfinished.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: internal native recovery capture now seals the exact physical tile references from an actual native scene through its authenticated source session. Store/reference bounds and shared resident/disk pools are checked before descriptor allocation; counting is cancellable, reference growth is admitted, and selected source owners remain retained through marker construction. No resident pixel projection is used. Regression source covers native 8/16-bit metadata and samples, masks/retained originals/selections, unchanged Engine history and saved revision, blank and newly painted native sources, and pending/foreign/cancelled/over-limit refusal.
