Compile-only checking passed; tests have not been executed. Main-frame ownership, native authority activation, remaining AI/proposal presentation and runtime/release acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: the Mask inspector now consumes borrowed resident/native document, layer and mask-step metadata, with admitted native settings copies. Cumulative native mask thumbnails reuse regional prefix evaluation over original channel sources, sampling bounded rows at native depth and ignoring only the whole-mask bypass for raw effect inspection. A source-bound sampling worker returns admitted 48-pixel display data; charged stock-egui uploads preserve pixel accounting through texture retirement. Per-layer caches bind engine/project, native generation and step IDs, retain bounded errors without frame-by-frame retry, and cancel on source/project changes. Regression source covers independent 8/16-bit prefix pixels, offset layers, whole-mask bypass, missing stages, cancellation, admission refusal, history/source preservation and source-owner retirement. Compile-only checking passed; tests have not been executed. Main-frame/layer-panel ownership, native authority activation, remaining AI/proposal presentation and runtime/release acceptance remain open.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: the Color effect inspector now consumes borrowed resident/native layer, effect and document metadata. Selected inspector fields are admitted before native copies; embedded bitmap data stays with its source until an edit requires a separately admitted command. Native pattern-picker drafts retain admitted settings and source-generation guards, reject changed effect settings and use the shared edit dispatcher. Native bitmap labels avoid decoding source images on the UI thread. Regression source covers native 8/16-bit inspector rendering, omitted bitmap fields, admission refusal/retirement and unchanged metadata; picker fixtures retain immutable resident settings checks. The Mask inspector and its per-step thumbnails, remaining frame ownership, native authority activation, raster/proposal presentation and runtime/release acceptance remain open. Tests have not been executed.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: numeric AI presentation now reads shared immutable metadata snapshots for layer opacity/fill color, effect/filter weights and settings, mask parameters and wand controls. Production numeric callers no longer clone a resident document for animation; inspector interpolation touches only selected fields and leaves source payloads and committed caches unchanged. The filter list and wand toolbar accept borrowed resident/native document views. Regression source covers both depths, real native metadata, exact observation/generation rejection, actual filter/opacity control rendering, source preservation, cancellation and omitted payloads. Compile-only checking passed; tests have not been executed. Remaining resident effect-panel/pending-gesture ownership, native raster crossfades, proposal presentation, live native authority and runtime/release acceptance are still open.

Unreleased scope update, 2026-11-07: [GitHub #59](https://github.com/Deftr0y/PeerBrush/issues/59) is tracked as PB-158 for the next version on `version/0.3`. Integration planning is underway for Scoop, Homebrew, apt and Nix, including package-manager-owned upgrades and preserved settings/documents. This extends the existing installer, update and release foundations (PB-145/PB-147/PB-154/PB-155). The existing DEB producer is a starting point, not a published apt repository. All four channels still require implementation, package verification, native install/upgrade acceptance and verified publication; the issue remains open. See the [package-manager scope](packaging.md#package-manager-distribution-pb-158-unreleased).

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: native canvas AI position, opacity and procedural-color transitions now use admitted presentation metadata over the exact committed raster roster. Derived scenes have separate cache identities and cannot become native edit authority or provisional import data; zero-source read permits remain bound to the original roster. The shared renderer returns display-only frames with source and exact-pose provenance. Native canvas request signatures include the quantized animation step; active human previews take precedence, and later observations cancel stale transitions. Regression source covers profiled 8/16-bit rendered parity, unchanged source/history, source-owner retirement, memory refusal/cancellation, zero-source permit isolation and stale-step rejection. Native numeric-control interpolation, raster crossfades, proposal presentation, main-frame ownership, live native authority and runtime/release acceptance remain pending. Tests have not been executed.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: the canvas now consumes borrowed document views for coordinates, gestures, selection outlines and tool dispatch. Magnetic-lasso snapping shares one bounded rendered-neighborhood scorer across resident and native sources, preserving display profiles and source precision. Native snapping uses the existing bounded sampling worker; clicked anchors stay ordered, only unstarted hover anchors coalesce, and closing waits for pending coordinates. Drafts and admitted native command data survive refused publication until retry or explicit cancellation. Source guards include engine/project, native generation and target; focus/tool/project cancellation and shortcut/update guards include queued anchors. Regression source covers snap equivalence, native metadata canvas input, pressure-independent coordinates, queue ordering, source changes, reservation refusal and admission retirement. The resident magnetic UI remains synchronous; main-frame resident ownership, AI/proposal presentation, live native authority and runtime/release acceptance remain pending. Tests have not been executed.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: Alt sampling is now connected to the bounded sampling worker and ordered canvas input queue. Pointer movement, pressure and completed strokes survive a pending sample; successive sample/stroke sequences use individual shared-engine commits. Receipt processing follows the captured frame before new work is dispatched, preserving the first brush press and avoiding replay of already-published pixels. Resolved queued strokes join shared-engine resident/native previews with admitted cached commands, while unresolved colors show pending feedback. Preview capture rechecks its actual source snapshot after intervening edits; dispatch reuses the admitted endpoint geometry. Queued Shift lines use provisional geometry and retain commit-bound endpoints. Focus loss preserves completed gestures; Escape cancels outstanding input, and conflicts retain uncommitted commands for explicit discard. Existing cache regressions now wait for asynchronous completion; added source covers gated pointer ordering, preview composition, manual overrides, frame/receipt races, pressure, masks, reservations, source changes and native metadata lifetime. Native authority activation, whole-frame resident lock contention, large-document responsiveness and runtime/release acceptance remain pending; tests have not been executed.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: an ordered sampled-input queue now retains completed paint commands and pressure data behind their color samples. It coalesces only samples without dependent strokes, namespaces reply IDs per queue, preserves separate stroke commit receipts and advances the source revision only through those receipts. A later source edit stops remaining input without offering an already-published stroke for replay. Commands and queue metadata retain physical admission, with event/byte bounds, mask-alpha preservation and explicit manual-color overrides. Regression source covers alternating sample/stroke sequences through the real resident engine at both depths, undo/redo, stale replies, source changes, bounds and native metadata retirement. Canvas/Alt worker integration is not yet connected; live native authority, test execution and runtime/release acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: brush strokes now record Shift-line endpoints from terminal edit receipts through the shared dispatcher, including queued edits. Pending endpoints retain exact document/revision, engine/project, target, constrained point and pressure; stale tokens, failed or cancelled edits and changed input contexts cannot restore an endpoint. Native endpoint metadata is admitted for its retained lifetime. Stroke requests carry the observed source identity, and resident/native previews share the existing live color/mask choice. Regression source covers both depths, masks, immediate/queued publication, reservations, cancellation, intervening edits, replacement engines and metadata refusal/retirement. Ordered asynchronous Alt-to-paint input, live native authority, test execution and runtime/release acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: the dedicated Picker tool now samples through a bounded worker using the shared resident/native pixel query. Requests bind engine/project/document revision, native generation, point and mask target; publication rechecks current engine state without blocking the UI. Busy-engine replies remain pending. Superseded requests, tool/project changes, Escape, focus loss and manual color changes cannot publish late colors. Native request metadata is admitted; completed replies retain only a pixel and request identity. Regression source covers both depths, unchanged source/history, contention, stale results, cancellation, worker failure and metadata refusal. Alt sampling remains synchronous pending ordered Alt-to-paint handoff; live native authority, runtime responsiveness and release acceptance remain pending. Tests have not been executed.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: pixel sampling now has shared resident/native rendering queries with finite, in-document coordinates and explicit mask-target validation. The UI picker and Alt cache use the shared resident sampler; native16 masks evaluate at source precision before display projection, using the renderer mask evaluator without preparing unrelated Color effects. Native queries consume admitted one-pixel preview stripes and return only RGBA, releasing scene/session owners. Regression source covers profiled composites, raw disabled masks, translated layers, a concrete low-bit native16 rounding difference, invalid coordinates, cancellation and unchanged source/history. Ordered asynchronous sampling, live native authority, test execution and runtime/release acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: native gizmos and Free Transform now use a bounded cancellable geometry worker. Alpha bounds reuse admitted native tile scans, preserving low-bit 16-bit alpha, hidden descendants and empty-layer frame fallback. Queries bind engine/project, native generation/revision, selected roots and mask target; completed replies retain no source document or session. Free Transform prepares bounds and Warp domains before enabling controls, with Retry/Cancel feedback and Escape/source/focus cancellation. Project switching and exit cancel outstanding geometry work. Resident controls retain their existing synchronous path. Regression source covers bounds parity, admission/work refusal, superseded jobs, source ownership retirement, failures and pending UI cancellation. Live native authority, other remaining canvas providers, runtime responsiveness and release acceptance remain pending; tests have not been executed.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: Warp target-domain queries now accept borrowed resident or native document views through one shared API. Native queries reuse retained-source support geometry, including off-frame originals, linked paint masks, hidden folder descendants and selected regions, without raster IO or document copies. Temporary metadata is admitted before allocation, cancellation releases it, and bounds are reduced without retaining a per-plane vector. Free Transform uses the shared query. Regression source covers both depths, affine/projective/Warp placement, mask linking, duplicate targets, unsupported targets and cancellation/admission refusal. Native canvas content-bound preparation and live authority integration, test execution and runtime/release acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: native preview frames now carry admitted selection bounds, polygons and contours through texture publication. The outline follows the displayed candidate pixels, including cleared selections, without retaining the source document, coverage raster or worker session. Borrowed document views expose active contours; resident cleared previews also suppress committed outlines. Regression source covers metadata sharing/copy admission, cancellation/refusal cleanup, stale bounds and native/resident presentation. Live native authority, remaining canvas providers, test execution and runtime/release acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: Pen targeting, hit testing and tool/canvas controls now use borrowed document/layer/source views. Handle prediction copies only a bounded path definition and reuses shared path creation/update validation; it no longer clones or rasterizes a document solely to draw anchors and handles. The actual artwork preview remains in the shared renderer. Pen commands bind document identity/revision, and successful queued creation selects the new path/anchor only after publication. Regression source covers native/resident metadata and hit-test equivalence, shared-engine control predictions, both channel depths, invalid/stale/oversized commands, borrowed targets and queued completion/cancellation/reservations. Live native authority, remaining canvas providers, test execution and runtime/release acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: bitmap-pattern imports now submit through the shared cancellable edit worker for resident and native dispatch instead of editing on the UI thread. Picker polling uses a nonblocking source observation, retains ready receipts while another edit is pending, and rejects changed documents/revisions before consumption. Picker capture now binds the displayed document ID as well as revision; immutable settings are shared across polls. Regression source covers held engine locks, actual queued submission, cancellation, reservations, stale documents/revisions, late human work, immutable ownership and 16-bit pattern promotion/undo. Live native authority, remaining presentation/publication integration, test execution and runtime/release acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: crop, canvas/image resize and selection/mask refinement now retain their drafts through shared guarded publication. Queued progress/cancel windows remain available, stale-preview cleanup waits for completion, and canvas reframing follows successful geometry edits only. Mirror/quarter-turn toolbar commits use the shared dispatcher and preserve controls while another edit is pending. Editor apply failures are separate from preview validation, allowing unchanged cancelled/refused drafts to be retried. Regression source covers immediate and queued success, cancellation, reservations, replacement documents, native-depth pixels/masks, preserved history, retry and pending-window visibility. Live native authority, remaining presentation/publication integration, test execution and runtime/release acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: Free Transform Apply and whole-image filter Apply/stack edits now use the shared guarded dispatcher. Each queued result is bound to its originating draft; pending controls preserve settings, progress/cancel windows remain visible through the lifecycle gate, and cancellation waits for the terminal result. Success closes the applied draft or refreshes the filter stack from borrowed metadata; failures preserve the draft. Filter editing accepts document metadata views, and transform application now checks document identity as well as revision. Regression source covers queued success/cancellation/reservations, replacement documents, unrelated completion tokens and original 8/16-bit content. Live native authority, remaining presentation/publication integration, test execution and runtime/release acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: folder creation, mask creation, layer reordering, visibility sweeps and canvas gizmo release now use the shared guarded edit dispatcher. Folder selection/expansion and mask-channel changes wait for successful completion; pending/refused work preserves existing controls and drafts. Layer drag and eye-sweep captures now bind document identity as well as revision, rejecting same-revision replacement documents before preview or commit. Gizmo publication uses its captured source rather than the currently displayed document. Regression source covers queued completion/cancellation/reservations, exact 16-bit source preservation and real layer-panel identity replacement; existing grouping refusal fixtures use shared-dispatch diagnostics. Live native authority, remaining editor/presentation integration, test execution and runtime/release acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: parameter release and direct control changes now use the shared guarded edit dispatcher. Control capture reads document identity/revision through metadata views instead of cloning resident documents. Pending commits preserve existing drafts and prevent a second control from rebinding while the first commit is queued; terminal failures retain the cancelled-control and selection-failure guards. Fresh keyboard presses can resume a cancelled numeric control without allowing held-key repeats or a held pointer to restart the old gesture. Regression source covers queued success/cancellation/reservations, preserved drafts, stale-source refusal, original 16-bit pixels and keyboard recovery. Live native authority, remaining native presentation and gesture/publication integration, test execution and runtime/release acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: layer and selected-pixel duplication now use the shared guarded edit dispatcher, including queued native completion and cancellation. Both actions bind the observed document ID and revision; a stale snapshot cannot duplicate layers in a replacement document with reused layer IDs. Completion selects only the returned created roots and updates layer clipboard mode after success; pending/refused work preserves selection and proposal review. Layer deletion root lookup also uses borrowed document metadata. Regression source covers nested root selection, queued cancellation, native 16-bit crop/copy preservation, document/revision replacement and reservations. Live native authority, remaining gesture/publication integration, test execution and runtime/release acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: native canvas source previews retain the editor's immutable admitted command and cached content digest instead of recopying or rehashing its payload in each canvas capture. The bounded preview worker assembles source and gesture commands in their original order before shared-engine preparation; completed replies retain no command payload. Pool/source/position/count guards, cancellation and allocation refusal preserve the original draft. Regression source covers shared ownership, reordered or changed sources, foreign pools, interrupted assembly, actual 8/16-bit source rendering and command/session retirement before replies. Live native authority, remaining native presentation, source-editor draft preparation latency, test execution and runtime/release acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: canvas requests now use a document snapshot and route native snapshots through admitted gesture capture and the bounded preview dispatcher. Capture preserves source-command placement, parameters, hover and shared brush/retouch/liquify builders; streaming fingerprints bind isolation, refinement mode and requested quality, skipping repeated command copies. Working metadata and serialized fingerprints have explicit bounds. Source cancellation releases its native draft, and matched terminal previews update editor feedback. Regression source covers command order, pressure/erase/tool equivalence, selection/mask refinement, admission refusal, unchanged requests and full-quality settling. Live native authority, native selection/proposal/AI presentation integration, large-source capture latency, test execution and runtime/release acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: the native canvas dispatcher captures admitted command metadata and queues source acquisition, committed/candidate rendering and texture conversion on the bounded preview worker. Replies bind project, source, revision, command/view fingerprint and cancellation epoch; stale replies and failures preserve the displayed canvas. Project switching clears native presentation caches, and texture retirement is reused per admission pool. Regression source covers original precision, source retirement before handoff, candidate-origin validation, stale requests, worker failures and UI refusal without retry loops. Live native authority, main canvas gesture routing, test execution and runtime/release acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: native selection cutout, mask and overlay display now belongs to the shared preview renderer instead of PNG response transport. Captured request tags bind the display mode; native workers and texture uploads consume the same admitted frame. Projection replaces one completed stripe at a time, preserves the original samples and publishes nothing on cancellation. Temporary edit previews project their actual candidate selection before retiring provisional sources. Regression source covers 8/16-bit texture pixels, PNG equivalence, candidate history/source preservation and cancellation after partial projection. Native UI authority activation, remaining canvas dispatch, test execution and runtime/release acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: keyboard and server chronological undo/redo now share guarded dispatch. Native cursor preparation, response admission and AI activity use the same observed source, cancellation boundary and authenticated publication fence as edits; old owners retire outside engine locks. Queued history blocks competing UI edits and selects no speculative result. Resident undo/redo now refuse revision overflow without moving either history stack. Regression source covers exact 16-bit preservation, cancellation, reservations, stale identities/revisions, actor ownership and native activity binding. The live native publication facade remains closed pending the single-authority switch; tests and runtime/release acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: general UI actions and inline renaming now use the shared edit dispatcher with document/revision guards. Native actions reuse the existing cancellable source-edit worker; resident actions retain immediate completion. Pending work blocks competing actions, preserves rename drafts and reports success only from the terminal result. Selection failures remain visible to the rest of their polling frame. Regression source covers queued creation, cancellation, stale renames and terminal selection failures. Native authority activation, remaining gesture integration, test execution and runtime/release acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: native JPEG XL import, placement and file paste now stream authenticated source bytes to the existing isolated worker and receive native 8/16-bit rows directly into staged tiles. Inspection binds dimensions/precision before child output allocation; original decoded-profile conversion and codestream orientation retain resident behavior. The shared supervisor now supports admitted row transport while preserving nonce/build checks, process limits, cancellation, owned pumps and uncertain-exit retention. Child callback/CMS/input/output memory is reserved before launch; confirmed exits permit verified retirement, while uncertain exits retain source files and staged rows for recovery. Parent-side full-image copies are removed; child input/output remain capped at 64 MiB pending incremental native decoding. Regression source covers profiles, precision, selectors, undo, malformed rows, cancellation, admission and cleanup. Tests, native authority activation, larger-frame decoding and runtime/platform/release acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: native WebP import, placement, paste and raw patches now decode authenticated source snapshots with admitted output/frame/canvas, transform, alpha, padded YUV, macroblock, Huffman and partition memory. Original RIFF/frame/ICC/EXIF inspection is shared with resident imports; raw patches retain first-frame channels without import color/orientation conversion. Shared WebP animation decoding now skips intervening metadata and correctly disposes the previous RGBA rectangle, including opaque and full-canvas alpha frames. Regression source covers both destination depths, selectors, profiles/orientation, disposal, undo, malformed streams, cancellation, budget refusal and retirement. Contiguous buffers remain capped at 64 MiB and decoding work at 128 Mpixels. Tests, larger-frame streaming, native authority activation and runtime/platform/release acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: the existing image-webp 0.2.4 decoder is vendored with its licenses and focused allocation guards. Opt-in limits admit lossless Huffman groups/tables and lossy VP8 partitions, including temporary copies and growth peaks. All WebP VP8 paths check embedded dimensions before plane allocation; animated ALPH additionally validates the following chunk type and padded frame boundary. Regression source covers static/animated alpha, malformed dimensions/chunks, declared allocation bombs and final-partition growth. Frame/canvas/transform/macroblock admission, native WebP integration and runtime/release acceptance remain pending; these options are not a total decoder memory limit.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: native JPEG import, placement, paste and raw patches now decode directly from authenticated byte spools using the already-installed pinned zune decoder. Native staging admits decoder scratch, original ICC data, progressive/separate-scan coefficient planes and the contiguous output buffer before use; decoded rows enter shared native tiles, color/orientation conversion and guarded history publication. Raw patches retain CMYK projection and ignore import profiles/orientation, while both resident and native patches now validate JPEG structure. Output and individual coefficient allocations remain capped at 64 MiB pending an incremental decoder. Regression source covers both destination depths, baseline/progressive/separate scans, profiles/orientation, undo, malformed data, budget refusal, cancellation and retirement. Tests, larger-output support, native authority activation and runtime/platform/release acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: JPEG imports now validate the original marker stream and assemble declared ICC fragments independently of the decoder, which can otherwise report a malformed profile as absent. Missing, duplicate, inconsistent, empty, oversized and late profile fragments are refused before conversion; complete fragments are ordered by sequence, including all 255 permitted fragments. Bounded marker/metadata/scan admission retains supported 8-bit RGB/grayscale and explicit CMYK/precision refusal. Regression source covers original profile conversion, malformed atomic import/place/paste batches, truncation and cancellation. Native JPEG/WebP/JXL producers, tests and runtime/release acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: selected PNG animation frames now reuse the admitted PNG pass-row decoder for native import, placement and paste, including base64 placement/paste. Adam7 passes, hidden default images, source/over blending and None/Background/Previous disposal preserve explicit frame selection and original destination precision. Resident APNG imports use the same row expansion and corrected post-disposal canvas, preventing Previous disposal from restoring pixels already cleared by Background. Whole-container CRC/sequence/frame bounds, source hashing, bounded decoder/work admission and guarded temporary retirement remain mandatory. Static PNG16 and raw default-image patches retain their policies; 16-bit APNG imports remain explicitly refused. Regression source covers composition, profile/orientation, selectors, undo, malformed streams, cancellation and budget refusal. Tests, native authority activation and runtime/platform/release acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: session-owned byte spool sealing now returns its writer on failure and retires only a byte-exact verified header/payload/index/seal prefix. Cancellation preserves the original failure reason and releases verified staging inventory; unknown partial writes, changed contents and foreign identities retain quota and report retirement refusal. TIFF encoded snapshots reuse this path. Regression source covers all seal/snapshot cancellation checkpoints and altered bytes with restored timestamps. Runtime/native acceptance and the full release gate remain pending.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: native TIFF import, placement, paste and patches now use authenticated session-owned encoded snapshots and bounded coding-unit buffers. Lossless strips/tiles, planar channels, classic/BigTIFF byte orders, explicit pages, original 8/16-bit samples, ICC conversion and orientation feed the shared native engine. Patches retain raw first-page behavior and legacy nonplanar CMYK projection. Native coding units/bands remain capped at 64 MiB and compressed units at 16 MiB; JPEG-compressed TIFF and extra sample layouts require separate admission. Failed byte-spool sealing retains inventoried data under the existing recovery policy. Regression source, native activation and runtime/release acceptance remain distinct; no release readiness is claimed.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: TIFF import now validates the selected page's original ICC declaration instead of accepting the installed decoder wrapper's missing-profile fallback on tag errors. Shared bounded classic-TIFF/BigTIFF directory inspection retains page selection, cycle/offset limits and cancellation; malformed, oversized or duplicate ICC declarations refuse before pixel decoding. Regression source covers both byte orders, native16 color/orientation, unaffected pages and atomic failed import/place/paste batches. Native TIFF pixel producers and runtime/release acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: native ICO import, placement and paste now preserve explicit variant selection and embedded PNG8/PNG16 precision. BMP variants reuse bounded palette/bitfield/RLE rows and apply AND transparency masks through staged sources; patches retain default-best-variant and raw-channel behavior. Selected PNG payloads have separate byte proofs within the held container. Regression source covers variant choices, both document depths, masks, orientation, undo, cancellation, allocation refusal and retirement. Tests and native runtime/release acceptance remain unexecuted; live native authority is still inactive.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: native GIF import, placement and paste now use incremental palette rows and staged interlace/disposal composition. Shared bounded container inspection retains explicit frame selection and ICC refusal. Static hidden palette RGB, animated transparency, zero-filled invalid palette indices and native16 destination pixels follow resident behavior. Source tests cover frame composition, disposal, clipping, both depths, undo, rejected choices, cancellation, memory refusal and retry. Native GIF patches now use raw first-frame pixels, including hidden palette RGB, with the existing unmanaged patch color policy. Import/placement/paste retain ICC refusal. Added source comparisons cover profiled input, first-frame pixels, selection clipping, both destination depths and undo. Remaining codecs, live authority activation and runtime/platform/release acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: native SVG/SVGZ import, placement and file paste now use the existing bounded vector renderer. Import retains editable markup; placement and paste preserve their rasterized behavior. Held-file hashing, admitted markup/parser/renderer memory, static-choice validation and cancellation protect preparation. Native file readers now require absolute paths, matching resident image import. Regression source covers source retention, both destination depths, undo, malformed/compressed input, admission refusal and staged-source retirement. Native activation and runtime/platform/release acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: UI paste now retains its immutable destination frame, matching copy/cut. A shared observation comparison checks document ID, revision, storage kind and native generation before clipboard reads and again through the final edit dispatcher. Typed layer paste and pixel/image paste both carry that observation; resident pixel cut now uses it too. Public metadata-based paste requests remain supported. Matching retained clipboard pixels are borrowed rather than cloning native samples; PNG/base64 command admission remains alive through dispatch. External native16 samples remain intact when the retained copy is only 8 bit. File paste now accepts exactly one absolute path or base64 PNG through the shared command; resident paste reuses the decoded raster directly. Native clipboard file lists route to the existing PNG/QOI/Netpbm/TGA/BMP producers without resident decode or PNG re-encoding, retaining paste placement, native precision and staged-source cleanup. Native paste now participates in the batch import budget. Regression source covers file paste, invalid selectors, ambiguous inputs, budget refusal, malformed streams, cancellation and late reservations. Budget refusal/retry and mixed-precision regression source accompany this path. Regression source covers matching clones, replaced native generations/storage, refusal before OS reads and concurrent human edits during layer/pixel clipboard preparation. Library and regression-source compilation passed; tests, native authority activation, remaining codecs/clipboard limits and runtime/platform/release acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: UI copy/cut shortcuts now queue the immutable frame snapshot directly into the clipboard worker. Resident frames share their Arc owner and native frames retain their admitted source generation; pixel materialization remains off the UI thread. Layer-row intent, active-selection precedence, mask-step targeting, merged copy and cut behavior are preserved under the existing focus/gesture gates. Public resident clipboard requests remain supported. Regression source covers both depths/storage kinds and unchanged snapshot ownership. Library and regression-source compilation passed; tests, full native authority activation and remaining clipboard/codec/runtime/platform/release acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: native pixel-copy/cut workers now render through the shared captured scene, project one source tile at a time into admitted clipboard buffers and retain exact 16-bit words plus document origin. Temporary native outputs retire before any OS clipboard write; retained images and PNG/DIB transport buffers have separate admission. Cut observes source generation and commits only after the copy succeeds. Regression source covers original samples, rendered masks/effects, coordinates, cancellation, foreign/zero-source budgets, allocation refusal, OS failures and authority changes. Current clipboard limits remain 8192-pixel edges and 256 MiB of retained transport pixels. Live UI/native authority activation, larger-canvas clipboard work and runtime/platform/release acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: the clipboard worker now accepts immutable document snapshots for whole-layer copy/cut, retaining native source owners and bounded tree metadata without materializing pixels. Existing resident requests use the same queue. Cut verifies the observed storage/native generation as well as document/revision and establishes the clipboard marker before deletion; failed writes preserve the previous copy. Resident and native layer capture both require an explicit compatible copy of protected Photoshop sources. Regression source covers source ownership, original precision, marker failures, authority changes, protection and asynchronous request compatibility. Native pixel-copy transport, UI/native authority activation, cross-storage conversion and runtime/platform/release acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: whole-layer clipboard paste now uses the shared edit coordinator with typed resident/native snapshots, observed document/revision checks, reservations, atomic history and cleanup warnings. Native snapshots go directly to the existing source-backed layer-paste worker; cross-storage copies refuse until budgeted conversion is connected. Regression source covers exact 16-bit words, promotion, undo, stale targets, reservations, cancellation and refusal without materializing native pixels. Native copy capture, authority activation, remaining codecs and runtime/platform/release acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: clipboard pixel paste and pixel/layer cut now dispatch through the shared edit coordinator with observed document/revision, reservations, atomic history and cleanup warnings. Copy still precedes destructive cut. Windows reads registered PNG first, then the first enumerated DIB/DIBV5 before its synthesized alternatives; selection/read share an OS clipboard hold that ends before decoding. Unsupported precision/color encodings cannot bypass validation through the generic image reader. An explicit BMP pixel offset repairs the installed decoder's legacy V4/V5 DIB offset behavior. Regression source covers original-format ordering, PNG16, refused fallback and core/info/V4/V5 samples. Native copy capture, typed layer-paste transport, the coordinated authority switch and runtime/platform acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: native BMP import/placement/patch now streams raw palette/bitfield rows and sparse RLE4/RLE8 with charged buffers and shared source-backed orientation. It retains installed channel expansion, black skipped-pixel semantics, held-file/hash admission and atomic history. Native and resident BMP/DIB/selected-ICO guards explicitly refuse bitfields wider than eight bits per channel, which the installed decoder otherwise truncates, and calibrated/profiled color encodings. Regression source covers headers, palettes, alpha masks, row padding, deltas, early end, orientation, precision refusal, cancellation and undo. Tests, runtime/native acceptance, remaining codecs and live activation remain pending.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: native TGA import/placement/patch now uses bounded palette and row buffers, retaining the installed decoder's packed-color, grayscale/alpha and palette interpretation. RLE packet state spans rows; all four image origins use shared native orientation before publication. Invalid indices, excess packets, truncation and unsupported interleaving refuse. PNG/QOI/Netpbm/TGA retain held-file identity/hash admission, original-depth staging and shared command guards/history. Library and regression-source compilation passed; tests, runtime/native acceptance, remaining codecs and live native activation remain pending.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: native Netpbm import, placement and image patch now stream PBM/PGM/PPM/PAM rows through the installed decoder into original-precision source tiles. Held-file identity and byte-hash admission are shared with PNG/QOI. Header growth is capped at 64 KiB; parser I/O failures and cancellation remain fatal even when the ASCII decoder treats them as delimiters. Import/placement use shared precision promotion; patches refuse native16-to-8 loss. Regression source covers all seven encodings, MAXVAL normalization, alpha, bit packing, native16 words, rollback, reservations and undo. Runtime/native acceptance, remaining codecs and live native activation remain pending.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: native QOI import, placement and image patch now stream decoded pixels into original 8-bit source tiles before shared insertion/composition. PNG and QOI share held-file identity, parent ownership, bounded buffering and exact consumed-byte hash checks. QOI decoding retains only a row and its fixed pixel index; incomplete streams, excess runs and trailing data refuse before adoption. Existing precision promotion, selection, reservations, budgets and history remain in the engine. Regression source covers RGB/RGBA decoding, tile/row boundaries, hidden channels, both document depths, malformed input, cancellation, late leases and undo. Tests/native acceptance, remaining codecs/transports and live native activation remain pending.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: native clone/heal now samples composed masks and merged artwork, alongside raw paint planes. Bounded sampling windows preserve native16 channels and the resident mask/bilinear rules; native8 merged sampling retains preview color conversion and its complete-row rounding. A private snapshot reuses frame coordination when the destination expands, so source masks, styles and Liquify clips observe the same pre-stroke frame as resident retouch. Temporary sources retire before the draw seals. Regression source covers both depths, fractional canvas/tile edges, disabled/hidden masks, profiles, expanded frames, cancellation, reservations and undo/redo. Compile-only verification accompanies the change; tests/native acceptance, remaining codecs/transports and live native activation remain pending.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: native effect.bake and mask.step.bake now stage original-depth prefix pixels through the shared renderer. Resident/native routes share prefix eligibility, locks and frame rules. Adoption requires unchanged raw target, raw mask, filtered composite and isolated-tree native samples; inexact prefixes remain editable. Mask prefixes retain rounding required by later cached stages. Regression source covers both depths, hidden sources, styles, selection, cancellation, reservations and undo/redo. Library/regression compilation passed; tests and native acceptance have not run. Remaining producers, transport integration and live native activation remain open.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: native layer.merge now renders selected siblings or an isolated folder at original 8/16-bit precision and replaces its complete source tree in one guarded history transaction. Shared resident/native planning covers adjacency, merge-down, clipping units, ancestor locks and off-canvas bounds. Masks, effects and opacity are rendered through the shared native compositor; document filters remain separate and selection sources remain unchanged. Regression source covers original-depth samples, nested folders, adjustment backdrops, styles, transparent/padded tiles, repeated merge batches, cancellation, late child reservations and undo/redo. Compile-only verification accompanies this increment; tests/native acceptance have not run. Effect/mask prefix baking, remaining transport work and live native activation remain pending.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: native image.resize/resize now stages original-depth resampling, editable-source rendering, mask reframing and historic Liquify coverage together before guarded adoption. Resident and native routes share dimensions, coordinates and sampling math. Two-row source windows bound resize reads; unchanged raster frames retain their source owners. Regression source covers both depths, asymmetric masks, hidden channels, extreme scaling, rollback, cancellation, late reservations and undo/redo. Library/regression compilation passed; tests and native acceptance have not run. Bake/merge, remaining transport integration and live native authority activation remain pending. Temporary coverage owners now leave staging before sealing, so seal-time cancellation can retire both resized Liquify and color-mask fragments.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: crop and all nine canvas-resize anchors now have native command producers. They share dimension/coordinate validation with the resident engine, move layer placements and world-space effect controls, retain exact color/mask/retained-source owners, and clear active/previous selections in one guarded history step. Bitmap phases and typed Liquify coverage move with their coordinate frame; off-canvas samples are retained. Regression source covers both depths, clipping, masks, editable sources, locks, late reservations, cancellation and undo/redo. Compile-only verification accompanies the change; runtime/native acceptance has not run. Native image resampling, bake/merge, remaining transport integration and live authority activation remain pending.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: typed whole-layer paste now shares native source owners across documents, promotes only required color/mask planes and retained originals, and publishes clipping-preserving insertion with shared reservations/history. Native pixel copy now uses the shared regional/stored renderer for masks, effects, adjustment backdrops, global filters and original 16-bit channels; 8-bit copies use the same row-based ICC conversion as preview. Selected-pixel duplication consumes that rendered source, preserves selection and clipping, refuses empty pixels, and falls back to editable-tree duplication without a selection. Regression source covers source ownership, foreign-service refusal, precision, rendering, cancellation, late leases, rollback and undo/redo. Compile-only verification accompanies the changes; tests/native acceptance have not run. Native UI/OS clipboard transport, live authority activation, remaining command producers and platform/release acceptance remain open.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: static PNG `image.paste` is connected to the native command worker. It shares paste origin, destination and hierarchy policy with the resident engine, preserves current/retained color and mask precision during promotion, and inserts pixels plus any wrapper folder in one guarded history entry. Paste clears active selection bounds/polygon/coverage while preserving the previous selection source. Root-level wrapping now keeps the complete clipping unit together; insertion inside a folder stays above the unit. All moved members must be unlocked, and reservations cover the selection plus moved layers or the containing folder. Invalid/cyclic/overdeep destinations refuse before decoding. Regression sources cover both depths, promotion, nested/root/empty destinations, clipping, repeated paste batches, late reservations, cleanup and undo. Library and regression compilation passed; tests and native acceptance have not been executed. Native whole-layer paste, rendered clipboard copy, OS clipboard integration and live authority activation remain unfinished.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: native `image.place` now accepts static PNG files and base64 PNGs, shares destination validation and premultiplied sampling with resident placement, and stages new-layer insertion or existing-layer composition atomically. Scaling uses bounded two-row source windows; unchanged destination tiles remain shared. Frame expansion shifts every mask step, including disabled/unlinked masks, while changed pixels discard stale transform originals and no-op replacements preserve them. Native precision promotion, selection independence, layer order and history remain consistent with the resident route. Existing-layer placement now reserves the whole layer because frame/mask changes can affect more than its destination rectangle. Regression source covers both depths, promotion, scaled tile boundaries, extreme downsampling, hidden RGB/padding, fill layers, retained sources, failures, late leases and undo/redo. Tests and native acceptance have not been executed. Other codecs/animations, clipboard/paste, remaining producers and live native authority activation remain unfinished.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: the native worker now connects static PNG `image.import` to color-managed/oriented decoding, layer insertion and automatic 8-to-16-bit promotion. Promotion preserves current color/mask samples, retained transform originals, editable definitions, selection coverage and effect clips; insertion and promotion share one guarded history entry. Parent/name/coordinate handling is shared with resident import, and existing import/document budgets remain enforced. A shared reservation fix now captures the whole 8-bit document for imports, image/layer pastes and bitmap-effect updates that may introduce native16 sources. Source regressions cover all depth combinations, folder/root placement, hidden RGB, source preservation, rollback, late reservations, limits and undo. Native acceptance and live authority activation remain pending; other image codecs/animations and placement/paste producers are unfinished.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: native PNG import preparation now normalizes held, color-validated source fragments through exact EXIF rotation/flips and bounded ICC conversion. It supports original 8/16-bit channels, palette/grayscale expansion and completed Adam7 sources without a full decoded-frame allocation. Intermediate orientation fragments retire before returning the converted output; the document command still owns insertion, promotion, reservations and history. Resident raster imports now also convert oriented complete rows with one CMS executor, reducing conversion scratch and matching native SIMD-tail grouping. Regression source covers every orientation, ICC/native precision, tile boundaries, interlacing, unmanaged-color refusal and interrupted cleanup. This preparation component is not yet a live native import command; other codecs, automatic promotion/insertion and activation remain unfinished.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: native `document.settings` now prepares canvas dimensions and explicit 8/16-bit conversion through the ordinary guarded transaction/history path. Only stored color/mask tiles are converted, preserving sparse holes, hidden RGB and physical padding. A precision change discards retained transform originals exactly as the resident command does; unchanged precision retains them. Editable source definitions, effect clips, layer frames and disabled/unlinked masks remain intact, while current/previous selections clear. Native16 bitmap patterns still block an 8-bit downgrade. Regression source covers both directions, unchanged depth, subsequent painting, cancellation, malformed parameters, late leases and undo/redo. Runtime execution and native authority activation remain pending. Native import/placement still need decoding, automatic promotion and insertion coordination.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: `image.patch` now shares checked document-coordinate clipping and precision protection between resident and native workers. Patches respect their rectangle, selection bounds and polygon/coverage, preserve the existing layer frame, and refuse 16-bit sources in 8-bit documents or floating-point sources. The native producer admits held PNG files, stages native channels, blends only affected tiles, retires temporary decoded sources and uses ordinary reservation/history publication. Regression source covers both depths, tile boundaries, coverage precedence, rollback, cancellation, malformed sources and late leases. Other patch codecs still require native decoder admission; resident codec support remains available. Native authority activation and runtime acceptance remain pending.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: the native worker now prepares `pixels.replace` with admitted bounded decoding, shared rectangle/depth validation and exact 8/16-bit channel interpretation. The JSON protocol remains little-endian for 16-bit samples; typed resident backing remains big-endian. Replacement uses the existing atomic frame coordinator for color, mask and Liquify-clip remapping, and applies selection coverage once. The resident route now also clips replacement writes to declared selection bounds even when the coverage raster extends beyond them. Both shared folder-lock guards accept a valid sixteen-ancestor chain while retaining lock/depth/cycle refusal. Compile-only checks pass; new source covers payload refusals, precision, coverage precedence, frames, late leases, cleanup and undo. Runtime/native acceptance, live authority activation and other producers remain pending.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: destructive `adjust` now shares native8/native16 color math with the bounded primitive worker. The resident command previously changed every stored tile despite rectangle/selection reservation scopes; it now intersects the requested rectangle, selection and layer frame, applies polygon and soft coverage once, and preserves sparse holes, alpha and physical padding. Zero RGBA samples remain zero regardless of tile storage; transparent samples with stored RGB still adjust. Default settings leave samples exact. Native preparation preserves unchanged originals and uses ordinary guarded publication/history. Regression source covers region/lease boundaries, precision, sparse off-canvas tiles, no-op source identity, cancellation and undo/redo. Runtime execution, live native authority activation and remaining producers are still pending.

Unreleased source update, 2026-11-07: the native worker now prepares `mask.from_color` through bounded rendered-color stripes and the shared contiguous-selection worker. It preserves local document coordinates, the existing RGBA tolerance metric, replace/add/subtract stack behavior, disabled-mask raw values and native16 fractional samples. Temporary color/coverage sources retire before publication; revision, reservations and history remain shared-engine responsibilities. The resident and native routes share overflow-safe point validation. Regression source covers both depths, offset layers, effects, folders, fill layers, selection independence, full-stack replacement, late reservations, interruption and undo/redo. Compile-only verification is recorded separately; these cases and native UI acceptance have not been executed. Live native document authority and the remaining command families are still unfinished.

Development now proceeds directly on `version/0.3`. Routine push and feature-PR
CI are disabled; a small essential gate runs for version-to-production `main`
candidates. Existing WIP branches preserve unfinished work and remain unqualified.
See the [development guide](development-guide.md).

## Next-version scope and current increment — 2026-11-06

Legacy `fill` now has a native route for procedural recoloring, selected fill
materialization, raster/mask blending and folder-child insertion (2026-11-07).
The shared resident engine now materializes a selected fill's visible color before
taking the soft-selection baseline, preserving pixels outside that selection.
Folder children start at document depth, retaining fractional native16 alpha.
Procedural recolors and folder fills reserve their whole target because those
branches ignore the command rectangle. Native outputs use the guarded paint,
linked-frame and insertion transactions; transparent/empty folder fills retain
the legacy insertion behavior. Regression source covers both depths, scope
semantics, exact pixels, history and failed-batch retirement. Compile-only checks
pass; test execution, live native authority and native acceptance remain pending.

Native rectangle/ellipse and horizontal alpha-gradient commands now share their
raster math with the resident engine (2026-11-07). The native worker stages only
touched tiles, preserves 8/16-bit blending, applies soft coverage once, and uses
the same write guards, preview and history transaction as native painting.
Regression source covers tile edges, offset layers, color/mask planes, empty and
polygon/soft selections, undo/redo, reservations and interrupted staging. Commands
requiring layer expansion now use the linked-frame coordinator described below.
Compile-only validation passes; regression execution, live native authority
activation and native acceptance remain pending.

Native paint expansion now adopts its draw result, all raw mask planes, retained
original poses, editable-source placement and local Liquify controls/clips in one
transaction (2026-11-07). Painting, smudging, raw-plane clone/heal, foreground fill,
shapes and gradients use their bounded framed kernels. Mask edits on procedural
fills materialize only the old visible extent. Clone/heal clips oversized source
masks and destination halos to the actual reframed geometry. Effect/mask budget
checks precede adoption; failed staging or late reservations retire the complete
proposal. Regression source covers both depths, asymmetric masks, soft selection,
projective originals, typed clip owners, source/fill mask edits, previews and
undo/redo. Compile-only validation passes; tests and native acceptance have not
run, and live native authority remains inactive.

Native adjustment-layer creation now uses shared definition/placement rules and
the grouping planner (2026-11-07). Standalone, clipped, folder-contained and
multi-root adjustments enter one guarded edit. Selection masks retain the standard
black-fill/white-paint stack and actual fractional alpha, generated in bounded
native-depth rows; existing artwork and selection owners remain intact. Resident
adjustment planes now start at document depth. Regression source covers all tone
adjustments, hierarchy, exact mask pixels, rendered previews, undo/redo, layer
limits, off-canvas/empty selections, late reservations and interrupted staging.
Library/test-target compile-only and formatting checks pass; regression execution
and live native acceptance remain pending.

Native effect deletion and reordering now use the structural worker and atomic
history. Surviving settings, native pixels and historic Liquify coverage keep their
owners; roster rebuilding updates clip references without replacing artwork.
Regression source compares two distinct historic clips, native8/16 rendered
results through the session-backed preview path, empty stacks, undo/redo and
failed batches, including locks, late reservations and allocation refusal.
Native creation and settings updates now also have a worker producer (2026-11-07).
Inline Liquify coverage is admitted and staged with its retained originals, then
bound as typed clip sources in the same atomic edit. Bitmap styles use the admitted
PNG decoder. Existing stack, precision, frame/work budgets and target restrictions
remain. Regression source adds all effect defaults, bitmap precision, exact clip
pixels, settings/control updates, creation/undo and cancellation cleanup. The
shared comparison fixture now includes retained originals and embedded coverage.
Native Liquify brush strokes now share resident input/default/target rules and
retain the selected native coverage source directly, including its original and
placement, without staging duplicate pixels. Regression source covers all modes,
layer/folder coordinates, changing selections, exact clip pixels, undo/redo,
empty selections, stack/stroke/point limits and guarded refusals. Both reservation
paths now use Liquify's actual 40-pixel default radius instead of 10 pixels.
Execution, live activation and native acceptance remain pending.
Library/test-target compile-only, formatting and whitespace checks pass with
existing compiler/vendor warnings. No regression tests were executed.

Native initial mask creation now uses the shared worker. With no selection it
creates the standard fill/paint pair, sharing an empty source at layer depth;
with a current selection it creates one opaque paint step through the existing
coverage producer. Selected creation forces Replace mode and preserves selection
history. Existing masks are refused, and publication retains layer/selection
reservations. Regression source covers native8/16 pixels and metadata, selected
folders, soft coverage, undo/redo, failed-batch retirement and allocation refusal.
Empty large mask frames remain metadata-only; selection-derived work retains its
existing size limit. Execution, live activation and native acceptance are pending.
Library/test-target compile-only, formatting and whitespace checks pass with
existing compiler/vendor warnings. No regression tests were executed.

Native mask-step add/update/delete/reorder now use the structural worker and shared
defaults/parameter validation. Existing step identities, native pixels and retained
sources survive updates and reordering; deletion removes only the chosen step.
New procedural steps receive fresh identities and an empty source at layer depth.
Mask enabled/linked state and current/previous selections remain unchanged.
Updated settings receive their own admission, while equal settings share their
existing owner. Existing cache limits, including the native16 uniform-mask
exemption, and the 32-step limit remain enforced. Regression source compares
native8/16 composites and raw mask previews, source identity, empty stacks, undo/redo, failed batches,
locks/reservations, cancellation and allocation refusal. Execution, live native
activation and native acceptance remain pending.
Library/test-target compile-only, formatting and whitespace checks pass with
existing compiler/vendor warnings. Tests were not executed during development.

Document filter add/update/delete/reorder now use the native worker, shared
resident command validation and atomic history. Settings copies are admitted
before allocation; unchanged settings and the entire native raster roster retain
their original owners. Kind changes reset defaults as before, and document-wide
locks/reservations, the 32-filter limit and current derived-work limits remain.
Regression source covers native8/16 rendered composites and isolated-layer
previews, source identity, undo/redo, failed batches, cancellation and memory
refusal. This remains unreleased preparation; live activation and native
acceptance are pending.
Library/test-target compile-only, formatting and whitespace checks pass with
existing compiler/vendor warnings. Filter JSON size validation now counts through
a bounded serializer without allocating the complete encoded settings. Regression
tests were not executed during ordinary development.

Native layer/folder duplication now shares clipboard forest selection and allocates
admitted metadata headers while sharing artwork. Layer, effect and mask-step IDs are regenerated;
parent/clipping references, root names and Photoshop copy-on-duplication metadata
follow the resident command. Native rasters, retained transforms, masks, effect
coverage and editable-source payloads keep their original owners and precision.
Plain roots are copied above their originals; clipping units remain together.
Regression source covers native8/16 metadata and rendered results, exact source
sharing, undo/redo, failed batches, locks/reservations, memory refusal and unchanged
layer/nesting limits. Library/test-target compile-only, formatting and whitespace
checks pass with compiler/vendor warnings remaining. Runtime and native acceptance
remain pending.

Folder creation from selected roots now uses shared hierarchy planning in the
resident engine and native worker. Visible preorder, complete selected subtrees,
the nearest common containing folder, clipping units and ancestor/descendant
locks are preserved. Native grouping reorders metadata and shares all original
raster, mask, retained and editable-source owners; the new empty folder uses the
document's native depth. Regression source covers native8/16, exact metadata and
source identity, undo/redo, failed batches, late reservations and the existing
100-layer/16-parent limits, including actual rendered-preview comparison source.
Library/test-target compile-only, formatting and whitespace checks pass with
compiler/vendor warnings remaining. Execution and native acceptance remain pending.

Native Magic Wand, Quick Selection and Object Selection preparation now samples
the shared renderer's captured display pixels through bounded source rows. It
retains contiguous/per-seed flood behavior, noncontiguous color matching, corner
foreground extraction, feathering and selection combination. Wand parameter
updates replay against the original selection; runtime replay ownership survives
batch revision changes and undo/redo, while ordinary edits invalidate it. Replay
baselines participate in source retention without entering PSD metadata or render
keys. Regression source covers native8/16 artwork, ICC and filtered display
sampling, all combine modes, replay/history, cancellation and late reservations.
Library and test targets pass compile-only checking with the reviewed native
JPEG XL tools; compiler/vendor warnings remain. Formatting and whitespace checks pass.
This is unreleased native-worker preparation: live authority is still inactive,
the 16-megapixel selection and exact-preview limits remain, and regression execution
and native acceptance are pending.

Native PNG-mask import now shares selection coverage publication and history.
File/base64 decoding retains complete PNG validation and final file identity/content
checks; explicit placement, alpha/luma sampling and combine modes match the public
engine. Remembered selection state is preserved independently of active combination
input. Feathering remains a separate refinement command. Regression source covers
native8/16 artwork and PNG channels, exact coverage/history, late file changes,
cancellation, invalid inputs, failed batches and late reservations. This preparation
does not activate live native authority or raise document limits; execution and
native acceptance remain pending.

Native selection refinement now uses the shared worker and rendered guide tiles.
Merged sampling remains the default; explicit layer sampling uses the same
color-managed preview path. Changed coverage and previous-selection state publish
together. An exact bounded pixel comparison preserves existing selection geometry
and previous state when nonzero settings produce identical pixels; optional
staging retires the discarded output. Prepared regression source covers native8/16
artwork, offset soft coverage, both guide modes, unchanged selections, cancellation,
failed batches, late reservations and undo/redo. Library and test targets compile
with existing warnings. Tests and native acceptance were not executed; live native
authority and larger-document limits remain unchanged.

All unfinished backlog items remain in the next-version scope. PB-108 retains first priority, followed by PB-122 and PB-110. Implemented features with outstanding acceptance remain open; historical completed work is preserved. Installation/update/publication foundations retain their destination-specific gates and existing holds. This scope does not qualify a release or raise the current canvas limits.

PB-156/PB-157 source now adds Ctrl/Cmd+E Eraser selection with custom bindings preserved, and full-resolution canvas previews by default. File → Settings offers a persistent opt-in for interim reduced-resolution frames. Both ordinary shared-engine previews and cross-project transfers finish at full resolution. Refinement display modes use the same treatment for interim and final frames. A preference change invalidates queued canvas frames without changing artwork or history. Existing profile migration and update inventories include the guarded preference file. Focused regression source covers native8/16 output, original sources/history, persistence, migration and protected files. The initial all-target compile found a missing test import, now repaired; the library and test targets pass compile-only checking with approved Windows native tools, with warnings remaining. Test execution and native acceptance are pending. This is not a measured PB-108 speed improvement.

PB-122 source now imports the bounded normal/unclipped `nvrt` Invert form and saves it as an editable standard adjustment in PSD/PSB. Empty Photoshop raster records normalize to document coordinates; masks and native channels retain their precision. Both resident and Source36 writers retain original raster channels rather than replacing them with a baked adjustment result. Format 35 binds resident sources to the new standard layout; older resident saves and both Source36 layout generations keep their full validation paths. Save reports distinguish editable adjustments from raster/effect bakes. Independent fixture, save/reopen, malformed-input and older-layout regression source is added; execution and Photoshop acceptance remain pending. The following metadata work addresses additional blockers in the supplied regression; this does not close PB-122.

The final library/test-target compile-only check passed with the reviewed native JPEG XL prerequisites; existing compiler/vendor warnings remain. Formatting and whitespace checks pass. Empty adjustment channel parsing now validates raw/RLE payload lengths and complete empty ZIP streams instead of bypassing their encoded data. Tests were not executed during ordinary development. No packages or public release updates were produced.

PB-122 document metadata follow-through preserves validated inactive/advisory `Patt`, `FMsk`, `CAI `, `OCIO`, `GenI` and `cinf` blocks in standard PSD/PSB data. Active or unfamiliar definitions remain protected. Metadata-bearing imports require exact native composite agreement. Resident format 37 binds supplementary metadata to the standard records; Source36 owns the same payloads under its shared admission and history model, emits correct PSB widths and compares every retained block on reopen. Synthetic regression source covers native8/16, standard-only reopen, masked Invert integration, undo, native owner retention, malformed/active records and private/standard disagreement. No reference artwork is bundled. The library/test-target compile check passed; final source review and regression execution/native acceptance remain distinct.

Native derived rendering follow-through now connects admitted colour effects to the shared Source36 compositor, preview rows, export rows and PSD/PSB rendered layer/composite channels. Preparation follows bottom-up, children-first dependencies, including adjustment backdrops, clipping and pass-through folders; raw mask and original source channels remain separate. Supported kernels are Levels, Curves, Adjust, Color Balance, HSL, Invert, Grayscale, Posterize and Channel Clamp. Paint/fill layers also prepare Blur and Bloom from their full local halo, retaining one native source strip at a time and avoiding redundant raw pins. Derived pixels retain native precision and their shared memory admission. Folders and adjustments now support the same finite-support kernels: a conservative remaining halo expands raw and earlier derived stripes, then shrinks in dependency order without recursively re-rendering lower layers. Stripe indexes and pixels retain shared admission. Color Overlay, canvas-aligned Gradient Overlay, procedural and embedded-PNG Pattern Overlay/Texture, Contour and Stroke now use their regional style kernels. Cropped raster/fill sources expand exactly at the first style: earlier effects keep layer-local edges, later effects use canvas coordinates. Prepared source prefixes and expanded outputs retain separate charges and native precision. Captured previews now prepare smoothing-field styles and layer-aligned gradients through session-owned native stages. Full-origin smoothing and alpha reduction use the exact preceding stage; completed folder/adjustment inputs follow compositor dependency order. Prepared export and PSD/PSB row providers accept the same captured tree. Source-only providers still require explicit preparation for global styles. Liquify now uses captured inverse mappings and typed historic scalar8 clips through the stored stage provider. Weighted native samples and local/canvas frame boundaries are preserved; output tiles declare their own inverse source footprints. Editable text/vector layers now use their captured current native raster for preview, effects, export and standard PSD channels without resolving fonts or replacing editable definitions. Native source creation and updates now route through the shared worker, preserving public command defaults, native pixels and one atomic definition/raster change. Text-field resizing shares projection geometry with resident editing, reframes masks from their retained originals and translates local Liquify controls and typed clips. Creation uses the existing sealed layer insertion path; source replacement validates through the bounded native renderer. Focused regression source covers text, shapes, paths and SVGs at both depths, cancellation, preview abort, history and resized mask/clip fidelity. Whole-layer and folder affine source transforms now render through the same bounded provider and publish native pixels with their source matrix and attached mask changes. Moves retain the current colour raster. Native path creation, style changes, anchor insertion/movement/deletion/conversion, segment splitting and handle movement now share the resident geometry code and bounded native source renderer. Definition/raster publication retains shared reservations and history. Geometry growth is admitted before allocation; existing frame and coordinate validation remains shared. Regression source covers both native depths, default/offset frames, reflected and sheared source matrices, preview abort, sequential history, malformed batches and cancellation after a completed tile. Closed path conversion and geometric selection, inversion, clear and reselect now use the shared native worker. Scan conversion is shared with resident selection; bounded rows apply feathering and combination before sealed coverage publication. Current/previous selection snapshots preserve physical coverage origin, geometry fallback and no-selection semantics without changing artwork precision. Plain rectangle/polygon commands retain geometry; commands with selection options produce raster coverage, matching the public engine route. Regression source compares both document depths against resident coverage, contours, preview abort and undo/redo, and covers late path/selection reservations, malformed batch rollback and cancellation after a completed tile. Native selection expansion/contraction now shares the extrema kernel with resident selection and regional refinement. One output band and bounded source margins replace full-frame intermediates; maximum-radius margins split at the native reader limit. Zero canvas edges, soft coverage, radius defaults, feathering order and atomic previous-selection history are preserved. Regression source includes independent square-extrema oracles, cross-tile/max-radius reads, native16 kernel values, bounded admission, failed-stream refusal and cancellation after a completed tile. Selection-to-mask and closed-path-to-mask now append an opaque native paint step through the shared worker. Existing mask steps and enabled/linked flags, layer pixels and current/previous selection sources remain intact; temporary path coverage retires before publication. Regression source covers both depths and all combination modes, offset soft coverage, cancelled output, invalid batches, stack limits, late reservations and undo/redo. Native mask refinement now shares smoothing, feathering, shift, contrast and guided-edge kernels, with temporary color-managed guide tiles streamed from the same preview renderer, including global styles. Guide/style sources retire on success and failure; target masks retain their native precision. Additional regression source compares exact guide bytes and refined native mask values, and covers cancellation and invalid-setting cleanup. The library and test targets compile with existing warnings; regression execution and native acceptance remain pending. Image-sampled selection still needs worker integration. Regression source includes affine/reflected/skewed text, shapes, Beziers and SVGs, linked/unlinked masks, folder transforms, foreign-definition/depth refusal and cancellation after a sealed colour plane. Explicit native rasterization now removes only the definition through shared reservation/history checks. Regression source covers missing-font reads, saved pixels that differ from fresh rendering, both native depths, and rasterize undo/redo and lock/reservation refusal. Focused regression source compares both depths against resident rendering and covers cancellation, target/crop grids, export resampling and standard save/reopen. Library/test-target compile-only, formatting and whitespace checks pass; compiler/vendor warnings remain. This does not switch live document authority, raise canvas limits or establish a latency improvement; execution and native acceptance remain pending.

Native document filters now feed the same preview/export/PSD composite paths through bounded composite stripes and the existing regional kernels, including Blur/Bloom halos. Layer/mask previews, isolated exports and original PSD channels retain their separate semantics. Scratch, input stripes and completed output share admission; cancellation and allocation failures return no completed frame. Regression source covers native8/16, cross-stripe halos, crop/resampling, target bypass, original channels and admission cleanup. Library/test-target compile-only, formatting and whitespace checks pass; execution, live routing and acceptance remain pending.

Remaining implementation and acceptance groups:
